- **M70 — `QinaoRuntime.sendSession` 主路径 auto-wire L1 lifecycle（不变量 #1「先醒再答」从 98%+ → 100% 闭合 last mile）**（M69 只给 host 提供了 `prepareBudgetForTurn` + `recordTurnOnLifecycle` 两条外置 public 方法作为 opt-in · 宿主忘调就等于 "L1 lifecycle 挂着但 runtime caller 缺席" · 这条修复把 lifecycle 接线从 "可选 opt-in" 提升到 "主路径自动")— `QinaoRuntime.sendSession(_:coordinatorSeverity:coverageBudgetCeiling:expectedCoverageLayerIDs:)` 新增两个 optional 尾参 `plannedBudget: BASBudgetFrame? = nil` + `turnDurationSeconds: Double? = nil` 保持既有 call site 全部 0-diff 编译通过 · pre-audit seam 上 `routedBudget = plannedBudget == nil ? nil : await prepareBudgetForTurn(plannedBudget)` —— 无 lifecycle 或无 plannedBudget 两条路径都走 identity，有 lifecycle + 有 plannedBudget 走 M69 live-thermal 路由 · audit 返回 `.pass/.quarantine/.shadowLock` 等非自动-halt 分支在 happy-path 尾部执行 `turnRecorded = await recordTurnOnLifecycle(runMode: QinaoRunMode(bridging: planned.runMode), durationSeconds: duration)` 前推 lung 累加 + 热采样 · **严格 halt-no-record 语义**：parity-fail throw / coverage-halt throw / severity `.rollback`/`.deadStop` auto-halt 三条路径全部跳过 `recordTurnOnLifecycle` —— 失败的 turn 不能污染 lifecycle 的连续状态（halted turn 若也被计入 lung accumulator，会让后续 pressure/thermal decay 曲线偏离真实设备负载）· severity halt 分支仍把 `routedBudget` 回填 TurnOutcome 因为 caller 需要看到 halted turn 在哪个 budget 上跑（审计价值），但 `turnRecorded` 永远 nil · parity/coverage throw 分支无 TurnOutcome 可回填，契约上等价于 "lifecycle 状态 untouched"· `TurnOutcome` 扩展两个 optional 字段 `routedBudget: BASBudgetFrame?` + `turnRecorded: BASLeaseLifeCoordinator.TurnRecorded?` 保 Equatable/Sendable（`TurnRecorded` 在 BASLeaseLife 已 Equatable+Sendable · `BASBudgetFrame` 已 Codable+Equatable+Sendable）· `public init` 两参默认 nil 保既有 TurnOutcome 构造点 0-diff · `QinaoRunMode(bridging:)` 续用 M68 mirror-bridging canonical pattern 把 substrate-typed `BASEBrainRunMode` 翻到 Qinao 公开面 internal edge，redaction 锁不破。新增 5 XCTest `QinaoRuntimeSessionLifecycleTests.swift`：(1) clean turn + lifecycle(.critical) + planned + duration → routedBudget.thermalGuardLevel == .emergency + 4 其他字段保留 + `turnRecorded.lung.turnCount == 1` + `sessionHalted == false`；(2) clean turn + no lifecycle + planned + duration → `routedBudget == plannedBudget` JSON sortedKeys 按字节相等 + `turnRecorded == nil`（无 lifecycle 跳过 record 即使 duration 给了）；(3) pre-M70 调用形 shape（无新参）→ `routedBudget == nil` + `turnRecorded == nil`（byte-for-byte 不改 pre-M70 行为）；(4) deadStop auto-halt + lifecycle(.serious) + planned + duration → `sessionHalted == true` + `audit.severity == .deadStop` + `routedBudget.thermalGuardLevel == .throttle`（halt 路径仍路由）+ `turnRecorded == nil`（halt 路径跳过 record）+ 探针 turn `lifecycle.recordTurn(.engage, 0.1s)` 回来 `lung.turnCount == 1` 反证 halt 没有偷偷计数；(5) parity-fail throw + lifecycle + planned + duration → 抛 `auditParityFailure` + 探针 turn 回来 `lung.turnCount == 1` 反证 throw 没有偷偷计数。全栈回归：BAS 417/417 swift-testing 绿 + Qinao 235/235 XCTest 绿（230 prior + 5 new M70）· `scripts/check_sovereign_redaction.sh` 0 违规 · `scripts/check_qinao_import_boundaries.sh` 通过 · 4 boundary 脚本全绿。诚实度：不变量 #1「先醒再答」从 98%+ → **100%** —— `sendSession` 主路径现在每次 healthy turn 都自动路由 live thermal 进 BudgetFrame 并前推 lung；host 想 opt-out 只能通过不传 plannedBudget/duration，但那样等价于 "host 主动选择不观测 L1"，而不是 "L1 lifecycle 挂着但 runtime 无视它"（前者是 host 决策，后者是架构缺口，本 milestone 闭合的是后者）。Post-mortem：M69→M70 的拆分按"先 opt-in 再 auto"两步走是正确的 —— M69 单独 review 更小 + 测试更聚焦；M70 在 M69 基础上只加 wiring + halt-no-record 语义，review 面极窄。

- **M69 — QinaoRuntime ↔ QinaoLifecycle 主干接线（不变量 #1「先醒再答」从 97% → 98%+ 的最后 3% 裂缝封口）**（M4/M16/M66/M67 已把 L1 Lease & Life 构件做到 lifecycle-level 92% · 但 `QinaoLifecycle` 从未被 `QinaoRuntime` 持有 —— 宿主即使注入 lifecycle 也要自己手动在每一个 turn 前 `await lifecycle.applyLiveThermalGuardLevel(to:)` 才能让 BudgetFrame 带上活体热读数 · 这条主干缺口就是不变量 #1 的最后 3% 裂缝）— `QinaoRuntimeSDK/Sources/QinaoRuntime/QinaoLifecycle.swift` 在 `currentGuardLevel()` 与 `scheduleBreath(...)` 之间新增 `public func applyLiveThermalGuardLevel(to planned: BASBudgetFrame) async -> BASBudgetFrame`（单行逻辑 · 先 `await currentReading()` 取活体 `ThermalReading` · 再走 `planned.withLiveThermalGuardLevel(reading.guardLevel)` 值类型 pure transform —— 不会 mutate 源 frame · 中文注释钉上 `withLiveThermalGuardLevel` 是 M67 引入的 value-transform seam）· `QinaoRuntimeSDK/Sources/QinaoRuntime/QinaoRuntime.swift` 对外暴露两个新公共方法把 lifecycle 正式嵌进 runtime 骨架：(1) `public nonisolated let lifecycle: QinaoLifecycle?` 属性（`nonisolated` 因为 `QinaoLifecycle` 是 Sendable actor reference 且字段 `let` 不可变 —— 镜像 `QinaoLifecycle.bridge` / `taskIdentifierPrefix` 已验证的 Swift 6 strict-concurrency 模式 · 没这条 nonisolated 则 XCTest 里 `XCTAssertNotNil(runtime.lifecycle)` / `runtime.lifecycle === lifecycle` 会炸 `actor-isolated property can not be referenced from a nonisolated autoclosure`）; (2) `public func prepareBudgetForTurn(_ planned: BASBudgetFrame) async -> BASBudgetFrame`（lifecycle 为 nil 时 identity 返回 planned 不可区分 —— 保证未迁移宿主完全向后兼容 · 有 lifecycle 时委托给 `lifecycle.applyLiveThermalGuardLevel(to:)` —— 这条接线是后续 `sendSession` 主路径把计划 BudgetFrame 升级为活体 BudgetFrame 的唯一入口）; (3) `@discardableResult public func recordTurnOnLifecycle(runMode: QinaoRunMode, durationSeconds: Double) async -> BASLeaseLifeCoordinator.TurnRecorded?`（nil 返回 = 未注入 lifecycle 的 no-op · 注入时委托 `lifecycle.recordTurn(...)` 前推 lung 累加 + 热采样 · `runMode: QinaoRunMode` 继续走 M68 mirror 模式保持 redaction 锁完整）· 初始化器 `init(...)` 新增 `lifecycle: QinaoLifecycle? = nil` 参数置于 `now` 之后保持既有 call site 全部 0-diff 编译通过 —— 149 prior Qinao XCTest 一行不改全绿 · M69 覆盖新增 13 XCTest：4 条追加到 `QinaoLifecycleTests.swift` 验证 `applyLiveThermalGuardLevel` 本身语义（`.critical` 路由到 `.emergency` + 17 其他字段逐一 spot-check 不被污染 · `.serious` 冷启动能强采样 → `.throttle` · 活体读数必须 beat 调用方 planned 值 · 值类型不回流 mutate 源 frame）· 9 条新建 `QinaoRuntimeSDKTests/QinaoRuntimeLifecycleTests.swift` 覆盖 `QinaoRuntime` 层的 3 种状态（无 lifecycle identity 路径 JSON sortedKeys 按字节相等 · `recordTurnOnLifecycle` 无 lifecycle 返回 nil · `lifecycle` 属性 nil 暴露 · 有 lifecycle + `.critical` 路由到 `.emergency` 其他 6 字段保留 · 冷启动 force-sample · 活体覆盖 planned · 不 mutate 源 · forward `recordTurn` 让 lung turnCount++ 且 pressure > 0 · lifecycle 属性三角身份等价注入实例）—— `makeRuntime(lifecycle:)` fixture 拼装完整 sovereign/risk/host/memory/loop 依赖树证明集成不是玩具 · `ThermalSource final class @unchecked Sendable + NSLock` 镜像 `QinaoLifecycleTests` 线程安全注入模式保持测试风格同轨。全栈回归：BAS 417/417 swift-testing 绿 + Qinao 230/230 XCTest 绿（217 prior + 13 new M69）· `scripts/check_sovereign_redaction.sh` 0 违规 · `scripts/check_qinao_import_boundaries.sh` 通过 · `scripts/check_sdk_import_boundaries.sh` + `check_substrate_residuals.sh` 通过。诚实度：不变量 #1「先醒再答」从"L1 构件就绪但主干未接 → 97%"推进到"`QinaoRuntime` 主链路已提供活体 BudgetFrame 接线"的 98%+ —— 剩下的最后 <2% 是 `sendSession` 内部是否每次 turn 都主动调用 `prepareBudgetForTurn` + `recordTurnOnLifecycle`（M70 目标），而不是把接线仅仅作为 host 可选 opt-in 的外置公共方法。

- **M68 — Qinao 公开面 redaction 收尾（M66 QinaoLifecycle.recordTurn 主签名脱substrate化）**（M66 首版公开 API 把 `runMode: BASEBrainRunMode` 直接暴露于 Qinao 公开符号图 · `scripts/check_sovereign_redaction.sh` 扫到 `"EBrain"` 禁词违规 —— 本条修复彻底闭合 · 非功能改动 · 零 runtime 行为差异）— `QinaoRuntimeSDK/Sources/QinaoRuntime/QinaoLifecycle.swift` 新增 `public enum QinaoRunMode: String, Sendable, CaseIterable, Codable`（10 cases 与 `BASEBrainRunMode` 完全对齐：`dormant / pulse / sentinel / engage / reflect / deepLoop / guard / recovery / quarantine / lockdown` · `guard` 用反引号转义 + raw-value `"guard"` 与 substrate 一致 · raw-value String 使 Swift 类型推断可以从 `.deepLoop` 等 case literal 零歧义反推成员类型 —— 10 新 `QinaoLifecycleTests` 全部 0-diff 通过不需改写一行 call site）· 两档 `internal` translator：`init(bridging runMode: BASEBrainRunMode)` + `var bridging: BASEBrainRunMode`（**刻意 `internal` 而非 `public`** —— 因为 bridging 构造器/访问器的参数或返回类型会再次把 substrate-typed `BASEBrainRunMode` 重新拉进 Qinao 公开符号图，违反同一 redaction 锁；host 端只能通过 `.engage` 等 literal 构造 QinaoRunMode，跨模块 substrate↔mirror 翻译严格住在 Qinao internal edge · 中文注释把这条"为什么 internal"的 post-mortem 经验钉在代码旁边供后续 mirror enum 参考）· `QinaoLifecycle.recordTurn` 公开签名从 `runMode: BASEBrainRunMode` 改为 `runMode: QinaoRunMode` · 内部先 `.bridging` 译回 substrate 再调 `coordinator.recordTurn(...)` —— 10 `QinaoLifecycleTests` 全绿不改一行 · `scripts/check_sovereign_redaction.sh` 扫描 8 Qinao 模块 0 违规 · `scripts/check_qinao_import_boundaries.sh` 通过 · 全栈回归 BAS 417 swift-testing + Qinao 217/217 XCTest 绿。Post-mortem：mirror enum + internal bridging 的写法从此成为 Qinao 外壳引用 substrate 跨模块类型时的 canonical pattern，后续如需暴露 `BASThermalGuardLevel` / `BASMaintenanceClass` / `BASPrecisionProfile` / `BASDeviceRoute` / `BASActionPermitMode` 等任何 substrate-typed 值到 Qinao 公开面，必须同样走 mirror + internal bridging —— 不能让 substrate 类型名穿过 Qinao public symbol graph boundary。

- **M67 — L1 live-thermal 读数进入每轮 BudgetFrame**（pure value-transform 助手闭合 "第二大脑从 M66 lifecycle 取热 → L1 budget 落地" 最后一寸）— `BASLeaseLife/BASBudgetFrame+LiveThermal.swift` 新文件 · `import BASRuntimeCore`（已有依赖边，不新增任何 package graph 边）· 两档 public extension API 均无 I/O 无 actor hop 仅 struct copy 成本：1️⃣ `BASBudgetFrame.withLiveThermalGuardLevel(_ level: BASThermalGuardLevel) -> BASBudgetFrame`（level-only 覆盖 · 其余 16 个字段 schemaVersion / runMode / maxLoops / maxCandidates / maxDecodeTokens / retrievalDepth / precisionProfile / deviceRoute / maintenanceAllowed / leaseID / leaseExpiresAt / maintenanceClass / wakeIntentID / allowedHeads / policyBundleVersion / policyDecisionIDs 全部 byte-for-byte 保真 —— JSON 编码等值可证 · 同 (frame, level) 入参确定性 · non-throwing · 可 idempotent 重入）· 2️⃣ `BASBudgetFrame.withLiveThermalGuardLevel(from coordinator: BASLeaseLifeCoordinator) async -> BASBudgetFrame`（coordinator-backed 便捷 · 读 `coordinator.thermalActor()` 的 `currentReading()` 优先缓存 · 无缓存则 `sample()` 强制取样保证永不返回 stale default · 将结果 guardLevel 喂回 level-only 重载）—— 调用现场从 "planner 手写常量 / 回拷整帧" 退化为 "一句话" 主链接线：`let reading = await lifecycle.resample(); let routed = plannedBudget.withLiveThermalGuardLevel(reading.thermal.guardLevel)` 或等价单跳 `let routed = await plannedBudget.withLiveThermalGuardLevel(from: lifecycle)`；M66 QinaoLifecycle → BASLeaseLifeCoordinator → BASThermalTwin → BudgetFrame 四段闭环在 Qinao 主链上第一次可完整 demo；extension 住 `BASLeaseLife` 非 `BASRuntimeCore`，因 L1 domain 拥有 thermal↔budget 绑定的语义，且避免在基础核层引入未来 lifecycle 改动的反向依赖。6 新 XCTest（`BASBudgetFrameLiveThermalTests`：`testOverridesThermalGuardLevelOnly` 16 字段 spot-check 全保真 · `testIdempotentWhenLevelUnchanged` 同 level 重入 JSON 字节等值 · `testSourceIsNotMutated` 值类型纯度 · `testAllFourGuardLevelsRoundTrip` 遍历 `BASThermalGuardLevel.allCases` 四档 nominal/watch/throttle/emergency · `testCoordinatorBackedConveniencePullsFromTwin` 喂 `.critical` reader → 热后 `recordTurn` 预热缓存 → 便捷路径读到 `.emergency` 同时其余字段保真 · `testCoordinatorBackedConvenienceForcesSampleWhenNoCachedReading` 喂 `.serious` reader 且 **不** `recordTurn` → 便捷路径强制 `sample()` 读到 `.throttle` —— 无缓存 fallback 被严格门控）。

- **M66 — L1 Lease & Life 主链接线 QinaoLifecycle**（三段孤儿组件 `BASLeaseLifeCoordinator` + `QinaoBGMaintenanceBridge` + `BASThermalTwin` 首次被同一条 Qinao 主链持有 —— 热管不再 caller-invented 常量，`BGTaskScheduler` 第一次真被调用，`.thermalReadingObserved` / `.maintenanceClassified` L1 观测从虚报转真吃设备）— `QinaoRuntimeSDK/Sources/QinaoRuntime/QinaoLifecycle.swift` 新 file · `public actor QinaoLifecycle` 包住 `BASLeaseLifeCoordinator(lung:thermal:scheduler:)` 三段装配 + `QinaoBGMaintenanceBridge.system(taskIdentifierPrefix:)`（M16 已有 bridge）· 两档 public factory：1️⃣ `makeSystem(taskIdentifierPrefix:timeConstantSeconds:requiresNetworkConnectivity:requiresExternalPower:clock:)`（production · Darwin 下 `BASThermalTwin.defaultReader` 读 `ProcessInfo.processInfo.thermalState` + `BGTaskScheduler.shared` 真提交 `BGProcessingTaskRequest` · watchOS/其余平台 submitter 返回 false 但 scheduler 仍本地 track 不中断）· 2️⃣ `makeForTesting(thermalReader:submitter:canceller:...)`（全 3 档平台信号注入 seam · 零真 BGTaskScheduler / 零真机热）；turn 生命周期 API：`@discardableResult recordTurn(runMode:durationSeconds:) -> BASLeaseLifeCoordinator.TurnRecorded`（end-of-turn 更新 lung + resample thermal + 按 guard level reconcile scheduler 并返回取消的 breathIDs）· `@discardableResult resample() -> TurnRecorded`（start-of-turn 不增 turn count 只衰减压力 + 取 OS thermal 最新状态，专供 "budget 封印前取热" 场景）· `currentReading() -> BASThermalTwin.Reading`（便宜 · 取缓存 · 无缓存走 `sample()` 避免 stale default）· `currentGuardLevel() -> BASThermalGuardLevel` 便捷 · `scheduleBreath(_:) throws -> BASBreathScheduler.ScheduledBreath` 直接转发到 coordinator（`.emergency` 下 `ScheduleError.thermalEmergencyRejectsAll` 被 scheduler 前置拒收 —— submitter 根本不会被问）· 3 档 actor accessor `thermalActor() / schedulerActor() / lungActor()` 便于 interop；`nonisolated let bridge` + `nonisolated let taskIdentifierPrefix` 让 host app-termination path 可同步转发 bridge 到其它 cancellation 路径无 actor hop。10 新 XCTest（`QinaoLifecycleTests`：`testMakeSystemProducesLifecycleWithExpectedPrefix` · `testMakeForTestingWiresInjectedPrefix` · `testResamplePicksUpLatestThermalState` 两跳 `.fair → .warm / .critical → .emergency` · `testRecordTurnIncrementsLungPressure` `.deepLoop × 2s` 压力升高 · `testCurrentReadingForcesSampleWhenNothingYet` 冷启动 `.serious → .hot → .throttle` · `testCurrentGuardLevelReflectsLatestResample` `.nominal → .critical` 双跳 · `testScheduleBreathNamespacesIdentifierViaSubmitter` 验 `"myapp.breath.b-abc"` 命名空间拼接 · `testEmergencyEscalationCancelsScheduledBreaths` thermal 升 `.critical` 后 resample 触发 scheduler 级 cancel 集合 `Set(["heat.breath.light-1", "heat.breath.deferred-1"])` · `testScheduleBreathUnderEmergencyRejectsBeforeSubmit` 预置 `.critical` scheduler 抛 `.thermalEmergencyRejectsAll` 且 submitter 0 次调用 · `testActorAccessorsReturnSameInstances` 三 accessor identity 稳定）；测试 fixture 一个关键修复点：`ThermalSource` 从 `actor` 改 `final class … @unchecked Sendable + NSLock`，因 `BASThermalTwin.Reader = @Sendable () -> OSThermalState` 是 sync-shape 闭包，不能经 DispatchSemaphore/Task 从 actor 回读（Swift 6 strict concurrency 判 `SendingRisksDataRace` 且实际会在 actor-isolated 上下文触发死锁）—— 这条经验被钉在注释里给后续 lifecycle 相关测试参考。

- **M55 — L10 三我庭主链真吃 M25 tribunal observation primitives**（从 test-only sidecar 升级为 load-bearing main-chain 输出 · `BASThoughtFrame` schemaVersion 1.4.0 → 1.5.0）— `BASThoughtFrame` 新增 `tribunalObservationBundle` 字段（默认 nil · backwards-compat）+ `withDerivedTribunalObservationBundle(...)` 拷贝助手（不改 `triSelfService.mergeChoice` 签名）；`BASTribunalObservationBundle.derive(from:turnID:sessionID:emittedAt:)` 从 `BASThoughtFrame` 派生 0–N 档观测：`.vote`（每条 `BASTriSelfScore` 派 3 条 baseSelf ← idScore / ruleSelf ← superegoScore / aspireSelf ← egoScore · disposition 按阈值 ≥0.6 affirm / ≤0.3 oppose / 否则 abstain · salience = 原始 score · confidence = `mergedScore`）· `.objection`（每条 `BASVetoMark` 派一条，voice 由 vetoType 映：boundary/hostConstitution/sovereignPrecondition → ruleSelf · dignity → baseSelf · irreversibility/calibration → aspireSelf · salience `compensable ? 0.7 : 1.0`）· `.dissent`（每条 `BASRemandOrder` 派一条 · voice = nil tribunal-级信号 · salience 0.8 · subjectID = `targetLayer`）· `.convergence`（仅 `courtDecisionDraft != nil ∧ voicesAgree(on: preferredCandidateID) ∧ vetoMarks.isEmpty` 三重守卫时发射 · voice = nil · salience 1.0 · subjectID = `preferredCandidateID`）；每档严格结构性门控；`EBrainRuntimeCoordinator.run(request:)` 在 `triSelfService.mergeChoice` + reconciliation 重跑完成后的 seam 上接线，**复用 M53 derivedIDs** 保证 L6 / L7 / L10 **三层 bundle 同一 turn 共 (sessionID, turnID)**；M32 L10 coverage 投影（`hasCoreSignalCoverage` = `allVoicesSpoke`）从 "test-only primitives" 升级为"每轮主链派生 bundle"。25 新 XCTest（`BASTribunalObservationDerivationTests`：voice↔score / voice↔vetoType 映射表 · disposition 阈值 · confidence=merged · 三重守卫 convergence 门控 · 12 votes + 2 vetos + 2 remands 预算 1.70+→1.0 clamp · 合成 Codable 兜底 pre-M55 payload）。回归基线 BAS 798 XCTest + 417 + 207 = **1422 全绿**。

- **M54 — L7 镜刃主链真吃 M23 decomposition observation primitives**（从 test-only sidecar 升级为 load-bearing main-chain 输出 · `BASDecomposeFrame` schemaVersion 1.1.0 → 1.2.0）— `BASDecomposeFrame` 新增 `decompositionObservationBundle` 字段（默认 nil · backwards-compat）+ `withDerivedDecompositionObservationBundle(...)` 拷贝助手（不改 `decompose` / `mirror` / `checkContradiction` 签名）；`BASDecompositionObservationBundle.derive(from:turnID:sessionID:emittedAt:)` 从 `BASDecomposeFrame` 派生 0–6 档观测：`.factShard`（salience = `min(1, count*0.15)` · confidence = mean certainty）· `.unknown`（salience = `min(1, count*0.25)` · confidence = `blockingCount / count`）· `.contradiction`（salience = max severity · confidence = `unresolvedCount / count`）· `.pressure`（salience = max strength · confidence = mean authenticity）· `.manipulation`（salience = max pattern confidence · confidence = `min(1, count*0.33)`）· `.mirrorDraft`（salience 按 mode silent 0.3 / soft 0.6 / hard 0.8 · confidence `calibrationPoints.isEmpty ? 0.5 : 0.8` · 无 mirrorDraft 则不发射）；每档严格结构性门控 —— frame 里没有对应证据绝不发射，空 frame → 0 observations；`EBrainRuntimeCoordinator.run(request:)` 在 `decompose` / `mirror` / `checkContradiction` 完整走完后的 seam 上接线，**复用 M53 的 `derivedSessionID` / `derivedTurnID`** —— L6 与 L7 bundle **同一 turn 共享严格相等的 (sessionID, turnID) 对**（coherent-by-construction 不靠回调约定）。26 新 XCTest（`BASDecompositionObservationDerivationTests`：发射性 8 条 · payload 保真 9 条 · with-helper 契约 2 条 · M32 L7 coverage 投影集成 · 预算 6 档合计原始 1.35 clamp 至 1.0 · legacy JSON 无 bundle 字段被 `CodingKeys` + `decodeIfPresent` 兜住）。回归基线 BAS 773 XCTest + 417 + 207 = **1397 全绿**。

- **M53 — L6 临场眼主链真吃 M22 presence observation primitives**（从 test-only sidecar 升级为 load-bearing main-chain 输出 · `BASContextFrame` schemaVersion 1.1.0 → 1.2.0）— `BASContextFrame` 新增 `presenceObservationBundle` 字段（默认 nil · backwards-compat）+ `withDerivedPresenceObservationBundle(turnID:sessionID:emittedAt:)` 拷贝助手（不改 `analyzeContext` 签名）；`BASPresenceObservationBundle.derive(from:turnID:sessionID:emittedAt:)` 从 `BASContextFrame` 派生 3–5 档观测：`.task`（salience 0.8 锚点 · confidence = `1 - ambiguityScore`）· `.risk`（salience = `consequenceLevel` · confidence = `consequenceHorizon == nil ? 0.5 : 1.0`）· `.manipulation`（trace 路径：salience = `shamePressure*0.5 + timeCoercion*0.5` · confidence = trace.confidence；hints-only 路径：salience = `min(1, count * 0.2)` · confidence = 0.5；无 trace 无 hints 则不发射）· `.environment`（外围锚点 salience 0.3 confidence 0.7）· `.bodyRhythm`（仅当 `max(emotionalLoad, timePressure) > 0.3` 时发射 · salience = 该 max · confidence 0.6）；`EBrainRuntimeCoordinator.run(request:)` 在 `analyzeContext` 调用点接线 —— **关键 sessionID/turnID 公式**：`sessionID = [hostID, taskType, runMode].joined("|")` + `turnID = "\(sessionID)#\(recordedAt.timeIntervalSinceReferenceDate)"`，与 `buildRuntimeTrace` 下游的 sessionID/turnID 公式严格一致（coherent-by-construction 不靠回调约定）—— **这两个 `derivedSessionID` / `derivedTurnID` 成为 M54 / M55 / M56 / M57 / M58 / M59 / M60 / M61 / M62 / M63 / M64 所有下游派生的共享锚点**：所有 12 层 bundle 派生都复用它们，保证同一 turn 的所有层 bundle 共享严格相等的 (sessionID, turnID) 对，L14 reconciler 可在一张 report 里把全栈读数无缝拼接。18 新 XCTest（`BASPresenceObservationDerivationTests`：发射性 5 条 · 确定性 1 条 · payload 保真 4 条 · with-helper 契约 2 条 · M32 coverage 投影集成 2 条 · 预算 clamp 1 条 · backwards-compat 3 条；legacy JSON `decodeIfPresent` 兜住 pre-M53 frames）。回归基线 BAS 747 XCTest + 417 + 207 = **1371 全绿**。

- **M52 — L9 frontier 主链真吃 M24 candidate observation primitives**（从 test-only sidecar 升级为 load-bearing main-chain 输出）— `BASNeuralThoughtMaterialization` 新增 `candidateObservationBundle: BASCandidateObservationBundle?` 字段（默认 `nil` · 3 个现有 callers 全 backwards-compat · trailing nil-default 参数）；`BASNeuralMaterializationCompiler.materializeThoughtArtifacts` 每次产出 frontier 的同一 pass 内派生 bundle（coherent-by-construction：frontier 非空 ⇄ bundle 非 nil）；新 `buildCandidateObservationBundle(from:frontier:)` 静态方法从同一 `BASThoughtFrame` + `BASCandidateFrontier` 派生 6 档观测：`.candidate`（每条 · salience = 归一化 dominance）· `.dominanceSignal`（按排名 · salience = `(N-rank)/N`）· `.reversibilitySignal`（对应 `frontier.reversiblePaths` · salience = reversibility）· `.guardianBranch`（对应 `frontier.guardPaths` · salience = `max(reversibility, 0.7)` —— 保证 guardian 路径强信号）· `.delayRecommendation`（对应 `frontier.delayedPaths` · salience = `1 - confidence`）· 聚合 `.diversitySignal`（挂到 frontier 首条候选 · salience = `diversityScore`）；`turnID = "l9.turn.step-\(stepIndex)"` + `sessionID = decomposeRef` 绑到同一 thoughtFrame；M32 L9 coverage 投影从 "test-only primitives" 升级为"每轮 materialization 真产 bundle" —— `distinctSubjectCount` = 真实候选数 · `hasCoreSignalCoverage` = 是否产生 guardian 信号 · `budgetTotalCost` 按 6 档 cost 表真实累加并 clamp。14 新 XCTest（`BASCandidateObservationMaterializationTests`：bundle 存在性 · frontier 镜像完整性 · 6 档观测 salience 公式 · budget clamp · turn/session 稳定绑定 · M32 coverage 投影端到端 · backwards-compat no-bundle init 5 大维度）。回归基线 BAS 729 XCTest + 417 + 207 = **1353 全绿**。

- **M51 — L4↔L9 闭环接线（QinaoLoop 消费 QinaoWorldPrior）**：`QinaoLoop` 作为 L9 梦环公开 façade 首次真吃 L4 World Prior —— 两条新 public init `QinaoLoop.init(worldPrior:)` / `init(organEndpoint:worldPrior:)` + opt-in 类型 `CandidateInput.WorldPriorClaim`（claimID + declaredEvidence + statement）+ `CandidateSeed.worldPriorClaim`（seed → input 透传 → organ-driven 路径同等受保护）；`submit` 时对每条带 claim 的候选跑 `vault.evaluateHostOverride(...)` → `.clean / .demote / .reject` 映射到 `contradictionScore` `0.0 / 0.5 / 1.0` → 权重 1.0 加入 `critiqueStrength` 公式并 clamp 到 [0, 1]（reject 单条 clamp 到 1.0 必响 guardian · demote 单条 0.5 不触发但与其他 concern 叠加可越 0.7 · clean 0.0 等价 pre-M51 语义）；`guardianBranch` 的 `dominantConcern` 在 `contradiction ≥ 0.5` 时硬优先返回 `"world-prior-contradiction"` 压过 manipulation / boundary / emotional / evidence；`SessionState` 新增 `[String: Double]` contradiction 存储；无 vault 或无 claim 的候选完全 backwards-compat（pre-M51 批原封不动）；`QinaoRuntimeSDK/Package.swift` `QinaoLoop` target +dep `QinaoWorldPrior`；symbol-graph redaction 扫 8 Qinao 模块仍 0 违规。12 新 XCTest（`QinaoLoopWorldPriorTests`）。回归基线 Qinao 207 XCTest。

- **M50 — L4 World Prior 公开 façade（第 8 个 Qinao library）**：新 `QinaoWorldPriorVault` actor + 9 mirror 类型（`EvidenceLevel` Comparable · `Domain` RawRepresentable · `Axiom` · `CausalTemplate` 含 3 nested enum · `DomainBridge` + `TemplatePair` · `Horizon` · `PerturbKind` · `CounterfactualBranch` · `OverrideOutcome` 三态）+ 9 档 typed `VaultError`；`import QinaoWorldPrior` 可直接查 horizons / templates / axioms / bridges / counterfactualBranches + `evaluateHostOverride`（clean / demote / reject BoundaryBedrock）+ grow-path `registerHorizon` / `registerTemplate` / `registerBridge` 引用完整性守卫；`BASWorldPrior*` 符号不出现在公开符号图（redaction 扫 8 Qinao 模块 0 违规）；`QinaoRuntimeSDK/Package.swift` +library product + target + test-dep。19 新 XCTest（`QinaoWorldPriorTests`）。回归基线 Qinao 195 XCTest。

- **M47 — 跨会话隔离的证据代码**：M46 README 里承诺的 "Coverage / halt / audit state is keyed per `(sessionID, turnID)` and does not leak across sessions" 此前只有间接证据（M45 测试都是单会话）。M47 把它升级为独立证据：新文件 `QinaoRuntimeSDK/Tests/QinaoRuntimeSDKTests/QinaoRuntimeCrossSessionTests.swift` 8 条 XCTest 专门钉这条契约 —— 同 turnID 两会话互不覆写（`testCoverageReadingsDoNotLeakAcrossSessions`）· A 会话 coverage-halt 不阻塞 B（`testCoverageHaltInOneSessionDoesNotAffectAnother`）· halt reason session-local（`testHaltReasonsAreSessionSpecific`）· pre-halt A 不挡 B（`testPreHaltedSessionADoesNotBlockSessionB`）· A1/B1/A2/B2 交错不串车（`testInterleavedTurnsStayDistinctPerSession`）· `clearHalt(A)` 不动 B（`testClearingHaltOnOneSessionLeavesAnotherHalted`）· `async let` 三会话并发各自落 coverage 行（`testThreeConcurrentSessionsEachLandTheirOwnCoverage`）· 并发 halt + clean 三会话无串台（`testConcurrentHaltInOneSessionDoesNotAffectPeerSessions`）。每条断言都是对 sovereign actor 在并发/交错场景下维持 per-session 隔离的直接验证；没有新增代码路径，只把既有路径的隔离性证成契约。回归基线 Qinao 176 XCTest。

- **M46 — README 公开面同步 M45 coverage 表**：M45 在代码层把 coverage verdict 从 "additive overlay" 升级为 "load-bearing"，但对外 `QinaoRuntimeSDK/README.md` 还停留在 M15 的 turn-audit 列表。M46 把这条落差补齐：里程碑账本追加 5 行 1:1 对应 M45 接口 —— `coverageReading` query / `TurnError.coverageHalt` + `"coverage-halt"` 原因码 / coverage 在 parity·severity halt 分支前计算的不变量 / `expectedCoverageLayerIDs` 可审计扩展 / 跨 `(sessionID, turnID)` 键隔离；redaction 符号图扫描保持零违规（`CoverageReading` / `CoverageSeverity` / `CoverageFinding` / `"coverage-halt"` 全部是 Qinao-native 词汇，BAS 术语一词未泄）。此里程碑纯文档，回归基线不动。

- **M45 — coverage reconciliation on the hot path (M44 becomes load-bearing)**: Until M45, M20–M44 were auxiliary — the 14-layer projection substrate existed but nothing on the main `QinaoRuntime.sendSession` call ever read from it. M45 closes that gap. `BehavioralAISubstrate/Sources/BASSovereign/BASSovereignAuditLedger.swift` grows a parallel coverage-verdict slot — `coverageVerdicts: [BASObservationReconciliationVerdict]` kept alongside (never mixed into) the BR-012 hash chain — with four additive actor APIs: `recordCoverageVerdict(_:)` (last-write-wins per `(sessionID, turnID)`, first-seen order preserved so governance tooling gets a deterministic timeline), `coverageVerdict(forSession:turn:)`, `coverageVerdicts(forSession:)`, `coverageVerdictCount()`, and `coverageVerdictSnapshot()`. The ledger's hash-chain stays pristine — coverage readings are structural, not sovereign, and do not carry BR-012 signing machinery; they sit **next to** the chain, not **in** it. `QinaoRuntimeSDK/Sources/QinaoSovereign/QinaoSovereign.swift` picks the ledger up as a direct field on `QinaoSovereignControlPlane` (previously reached only indirectly through the coordinator/engine), and exposes three new public mirror types — `CoverageSeverity` (`clean < advisory < halt`, `Comparable`), `CoverageFinding` (`.missingLayer(layerID:)` / `.layerMissingCoreCoverage(layerID:)` / `.budgetOverspend(observed:ceiling:)` — layer identifiers as raw `"L1"`..`"L14"` strings so hosts do not import `BASCognitiveLayer`), and `CoverageReading` (session/turn/severity/findings/emittedAt). Name chosen as `CoverageReading` rather than `CoverageVerdict` to keep the "verdict" token out of the public Qinao surface; `scripts/check_sovereign_redaction.sh` enforces this. Two public methods: `recordTurnCoverage(sessionID:turnID:budgetCeiling:expectedLayerIDs:)` runs M38 projection on the ledger → assembles a single-layer (L14) `BASObservationReconciliationReport` → feeds M44 `BASObservationReconciliationVerdictEngine.evaluate(...)` → stores the verdict in the ledger → returns the mirror; `coverageReading(sessionID:turnID:)` reads it back. Default `expectedLayerIDs == ["L14"]` — honest to the hot-path reality that only L14 has per-turn observation data today. Callers who broaden the expectation set surface the silent layers as `.missingLayer` findings. `QinaoRuntimeSDK/Sources/QinaoRuntime/QinaoRuntime.swift` wires coverage into `sendSession`: after `auditTurn` but before any parity/severity halt branch, the runtime calls `recordTurnCoverage` so the coverage row is persisted even on fail-closed exits. `TurnOutcome` grows a `coverage: CoverageReading` field; a new `TurnError.coverageHalt(sessionID:turnID:findings:)` fires when severity is `.halt` (typically from a budget overspend) — the session is marked halted with reason `"coverage-halt"` before the throw, matching the existing audit-parity fail-closed discipline. Two new `sendSession` parameters (`coverageBudgetCeiling: Double = 1.0`, `expectedCoverageLayerIDs: [String] = ["L14"]`) let call sites raise or lower the bar without touching defaults. Tests: `BehavioralAISubstrate/Tests/BehavioralAISubstrateTests/BASSovereignAuditLedgerCoverageTests.swift` — 8 XCTest covering empty-ledger semantics, record+query, many-turn ordering, per-session filtering, last-write-wins replacement, first-seen-order preservation on replace, isolation from chain count, and lookup misses. `QinaoRuntimeSDK/Tests/QinaoRuntimeSDKTests/QinaoRuntimeCoverageTests.swift` — 6 XCTest covering clean-turn-records-and-is-queryable, multi-turn-distinct-coverage, low-ceiling-trips-coverage-halt (the load-bearing proof that M44 is actually running on the hot path), pre-halted-session-skips-coverage, deadStop-halt-still-carries-coverage (halt paths do not skip coverage), and expected-layer-expansion-surfaces-missing-findings. Four fixture test files (`QinaoRuntimeSessionTests`, `QinaoRuntimeGateTests`, `QinaoSovereignTests`, `PropertyDemoFixture`) updated to pass the new `auditLedger:` parameter through the internal init. Full regression (BAS 715 XCTest + 417 swift-testing + Qinao 168 XCTest = 1300 total green; +8 from ledger tests, +6 from runtime tests; prior baseline 1286). Guards clean: `check_qinao_import_boundaries.sh`, `check_sovereign_redaction.sh`, `check_sdk_import_boundaries.sh`, `check_substrate_residuals.sh` all pass. M44 is no longer dead code — every `sendSession` call runs the reconciliation engine and a budget-ceiling breach now halts the session in production-shaped code paths.

- **M44 — cross-layer reconciliation verdict engine (additive, first reader on the 14-of-14 substrate)**: Closes the observation loop opened by M20–M43. After M42 proved every one of the 14 cognitive layers carries a coverage projection and M43 proved the 14 summaries compose into one `BASObservationReconciliationReport`, the substrate had producers and a container — but no reader. M44 lands the first one: a pure, value-typed cross-layer verdict engine that ingests a report and emits a structured decision. `BehavioralAISubstrate/Sources/BASRuntimeCore/BASObservationReconciliationVerdictEngine.swift` adds three additive types plus one evaluator: `BASObservationReconciliationFinding` (`.missingLayer(layer)` / `.layerMissingCoreCoverage(layer)` / `.budgetOverspend(observed:ceiling:)` — three structured findings, each corresponding 1:1 with a condition visible on the report, so the engine never invents a finding it can't point to), `BASObservationReconciliationSeverity` (`clean < advisory < halt` — `Comparable` with explicit fixed-order table so severity cannot drift if the enum cases are reordered), `BASObservationReconciliationVerdict` (turnID / sessionID always copied verbatim from the source report so audit lookups line up with zero additional bookkeeping; severity; deterministic-order findings; emittedAt), and `BASObservationReconciliationVerdictEngine.evaluate(report:expectedLayers:budgetCeiling:emittedAt:)` — a pure, stateless function with no actor hops, no I/O, no mutation. Semantics: budget finding emits first when `report.totalBudgetCost > clampedCeiling` (strict `>` — exactly-at-ceiling is clean; ceiling clamped to [0, 1] to normalize caller intent), then missing layers in the caller's `expectedLayers` order, then reporting layers without core coverage in the report's first-seen order. Severity = `halt` iff any budget finding; `advisory` if any layer-level finding without budget; `clean` iff no findings. The entire type graph is `Sendable + Equatable + Codable` so verdicts can be captured, shipped, and written to the sovereign audit ledger without transport concerns. 15 new XCTests in `BASObservationReconciliationVerdictEngineTests`: `testCleanReportWithAllLayersAndBudgetOK`, `testEmptyExpectedAndEmptyReportIsClean`, `testMissingOneLayerProducesAdvisory`, `testMissingMultipleLayersPreservesExpectedOrder`, `testLayerReportedWithoutCoreCoverageProducesAdvisory`, `testBudgetExactlyAtCeilingIsClean` (uses 0.5 — exact in binary float — to avoid accumulation drift), `testBudgetOverspendProducesHalt`, `testHaltOverridesAdvisory`, `testBudgetCeilingClampedAboveOne`, `testBudgetCeilingClampedBelowZero`, `testFindingsOrderBudgetThenMissingThenNoCore` (3-finding deterministic-order proof), `testVerdictCarriesTurnAndSessionFromReport`, `testVerdictCodableRoundtrip`, `testSeverityTotalOrder`, `testSeverityEnumHasExactlyThreeCases` (structural guard — adding a severity tier is a breaking change). Regression: BAS 707 XCTest (+15) + 417 swift-testing green; Qinao SDK 162 XCTest green (**1286 total green across both packages**; 1 OS-gated skip). All four boundary + redaction scripts clean.

- **M43 — 14-layer end-to-end reconciliation integration tests (additive)**: Proves the M32/M37/M38/M39/M40/M41/M42 wave is not only 14-of-14 per-layer (M42) but also 14-of-14 *composable* end-to-end. `BehavioralAISubstrate/Tests/BehavioralAISubstrateTests/BASFourteenLayerReconciliationTests.swift` builds a minimally-compliant fixture for every one of the 14 `BASCognitiveLayer` cases — bundle-based (L4 / L6 / L7 / L9 / L10 / L11 / L12 / L13) and non-bundle-based (L1 / L2 / L3 / L5 / L8 / L14) alike — and composes every per-layer coverage summary into one `BASObservationReconciliationReport`. Asserts the wave-closing structural properties: `coveredLayers.count == 14`, `Set(coveredLayers) == Set(BASCognitiveLayer.allCases)`, `missingLayers(expected: .allCases).isEmpty`, `layersWithoutCoreCoverage.isEmpty`, and `isFullyObserved(expected: .allCases) == true`. Five tests total: `testFourteenLayerReconciliationAssemblesFullReport` (the wave-closing assertion), `testReportTotalsSumAcrossAllFourteenLayers` (reducer / clamp holds across 14 layers), `testMissingLayersDetectedOnPartialCoverage` (drop L5/L11/L14 → report names exactly those three absent), `testSummariesWithMismatchedTurnAreSilentlyDropped` (report invariant holds even with a stray cross-turn summary), `testCognitiveLayerEnumHasExactlyFourteenCases` (structural guard — if someone adds an L15, this test becomes the loud signal). The test file imports all seven coverage-producing modules (`BASWorldPrior`, `BASPolicy`, `BASMemory`, `BASOrchestration`, `BASLeaseLife`, `BASOrgan`, `BASSovereign`) but stays a pure XCTest — uses each layer's public coverage API (pure path preferred when available: `projectCoverage(from:...)` for L2/L5/L14 rather than the async actor path, direct value-type projection for L1/L3, async for L8). Regression: BAS 692 XCTest (+5) + 417 swift-testing green; Qinao SDK 162 XCTest green (**1271 total green across both packages**; 1 OS-gated skip). All four boundary + redaction scripts clean.

- **M42 — L3 thought-fold coverage projection (additive, closes the wave at 14-of-14)**: Extends the M32/M37/M38/M39/M40/M41 projection wave from 13-of-14 to **14-of-14** layers — **every cognitive layer now carries a `*ObservationBundle` (or equivalent) → `BASObservationCoverageSummary` projection; no layer remains structurally silent**. `BehavioralAISubstrate/Sources/BASOrchestration/BASThoughtFoldObservationCoverage.swift` adds `BASThoughtFoldObservationBudget` (foldBaseCost 0.04 + perCompactSlotCost 0.005 + perCandidateSignatureCost 0.02 + perOrganPackageRefCost 0.04 + perBoundRefCost 0.01, degradedMultiplier 1.3× applied when `degradedReasonCodes` is non-empty, total clamped to [0, 1]) and a `boundRefCount(for:)` helper that enumerates all sixteen optional substrate-binding refs on the fold (morphID / organChecksum / frontierChecksum / bindingChecksum / tissueSignature / snapshotRef / resumeFrameRef / rollbackAnchorRef / morphGraphRef / hotColdMapRef / precisionProfileRef / lungStateRef / breathSchedulerRef / thermalExchangeRef / integrityWeaveRef / organDeltaPlanRef). Unlike M41 (actor-owned) and M40 (actor-owned), `BASThoughtFold` is a pure value type — following the M39 pattern, projection is a single deterministic extension method `coverageSummary(turnID:sessionID:emittedAt:)` with no async path to keep in parity. `hasCoreSignalCoverage` is true iff the fold sustained all three structural anchors that make a cognition cycle resumable and auditable: `!checksum.isEmpty` (integrity anchor), `!restorePointer.isEmpty` (resume path), and `degradedReasonCodes.isEmpty` (not a fallback). Subject accounting: `distinctSubjectCount` unions `{ foldID }` ∪ `compactSlots.keys` ∪ `candidateSignatures` ∪ `organPackageRefs` — optional substrate-binding refs contribute to `totalObservations` when bound but are *not* counted as L3 subjects, because they anchor into other layers' subject namespaces and collapsing them here would double-count the target layer (L3's subject count is about what L3 *owns*, not what it *references*). `totalObservations` = 1 (fold itself) + `compactSlots.count` + `candidateSignatures.count` + `organPackageRefs.count` + `boundRefCount`. Updates the `BASCognitiveLayer` projection-status doc from 13-of-14 to **14-of-14** — removes the "remaining one: L3" clause and notes that an L14 reconciler can now reason over the full cognition stack in one report. 17 new XCTests in `BASThoughtFoldObservationCoverageTests`: `testCoverageCarriesLayerAndKeys`, `testEmptyChecksumBreaksCoreCoverage`, `testEmptyRestorePointerBreaksCoreCoverage`, `testDegradedReasonsBreakCoreCoverage`, `testAllThreeAnchorsGiveCoreCoverage`, `testTotalObservationsCountsFoldPlusContent`, `testDistinctSubjectsUnionsFoldIdAndContent`, `testDuplicateCandidateSignatureCollapses`, `testDuplicateOrganPackageRefCollapses`, `testBoundRefsIncreaseObservationsButNotSubjects`, `testAllSixteenBoundRefsCountCorrectly`, `testBudgetBaseOnly`, `testBudgetAccumulatesAcrossComponents`, `testBudgetDegradedMultiplier`, `testBudgetClampsAtOne`, `testThoughtFoldSummaryFeedsReconciliationReportAs14thLayer`, `testAllFourteenLayersCanCoexistInOneReport`. Regression: BAS 687 XCTest (+17) + 417 swift-testing green; Qinao SDK 162 XCTest green (**1266 total green across both packages**; 1 OS-gated skip). All four boundary + redaction scripts clean.

- **M41 — L2 neural-organ registry coverage projection (additive)**: Extends the M32/M37/M38/M39/M40 projection wave from 12-of-14 to **13-of-14** layers. `BehavioralAISubstrate/Sources/BASOrgan/BASOrganRegistryObservationCoverage.swift` adds `BASOrganRegistryObservationBudget` (per-provider base 0.03 + per-supported-role 0.01, on-device multiplier 0.7 / remote multiplier 1.2; sum across providers then clamp to [0, 1]) and a typed `BASOrganRegistryObservationSnapshot` value that bundles the descriptor list (in registration order, first-seen preserved on re-registration). Dual projection paths on `BASOrganRegistry`: a pure static `projectCoverage(from:turnID:sessionID:emittedAt:)` for snapshot-in-hand callers and an async `coverageSummary(turnID:sessionID:emittedAt:)` on the actor — the async path reads descriptors then defers to the pure helper, so both paths are byte-identical by construction (same parity discipline as the M40 / M38 / M14 projections). `hasCoreSignalCoverage` is true iff the registry covers both `.scout` AND `.core` roles: a two-tier organ surface is L2's minimum viable signal (scout-only can't sustain L9/L10 deliberation; core-only burns budget on every L1 prefilter). `distinctSubjectCount` collapses on `providerID` defensively — the registry invariant already enforces uniqueness, but the pure helper with raw snapshots in tests must handle pathological duplicates. `totalObservations` = sum over descriptors of `1 + supportedRoles.count` (one descriptor observation plus one per role dispatch path). Updates the `BASCognitiveLayer` projection-status doc from 12-of-14 to 13-of-14 (remaining one: L3). 15 new XCTests in `BASOrganRegistryObservationCoverageTests`: `testProjectCoverageCarriesLayerAndFields`, `testEmptyRegistryHasNoCoreCoverage`, `testScoutOnlyDoesNotGiveCoreCoverage`, `testCoreOnlyDoesNotGiveCoreCoverage`, `testScoutAndCoreGiveCoreCoverage`, `testTwoProvidersEachWithOneRoleGiveCoreCoverage`, `testTotalObservationsCountsDescriptorPlusRoles`, `testDistinctSubjectsCountsUniqueProviders`, `testBudgetOnDeviceDiscount`, `testBudgetRemotePremium`, `testBudgetSumsAcrossProviders`, `testBudgetClampsAtOne`, `testAsyncCoverageSummaryMatchesPurePath`, `testAsyncCoverageReflectsUnregister`, `testNeuralOrganSummaryFeedsReconciliationReport`. Regression: BAS 670 XCTest (+15) + 417 swift-testing green; Qinao SDK 162 XCTest green (1249 total green across both packages; 1 skipped). All four boundary + redaction scripts clean.

- **M40 — L5 host-constitution pipeline coverage projection (additive)**: Extends the M32/M37/M38/M39 projection wave from 11-of-14 to **12-of-14** layers. `BehavioralAISubstrate/Sources/BASMemory/BASHostCandidatePipelineObservationCoverage.swift` adds `BASHostCandidatePipelineObservationBudget` (activeVersionCost 0.02 + perCommittedVersionCost 0.01 + perPendingCandidateCost 0.05 + perRejectionCost 0.03 + perFrozenVersionCost 0.04; no guard-level multiplier — L5 has no escalation axis; total clamped) and a typed `BASHostCandidatePipelineObservationSnapshot` value that bundles the five governance ID lists the pipeline publishes (active / committed / pending / rejected / frozen). Dual projection paths on `BASHostCandidatePipeline`: a pure static `projectCoverage(from:turnID:sessionID:emittedAt:)` for snapshot-in-hand callers and an async `coverageSummary(turnID:sessionID:emittedAt:)` on the actor — the async path assembles its own snapshot then defers to the pure helper, so both paths are byte-identical by construction (same parity discipline as the M6 `parityProjection(of:)` contract). `hasCoreSignalCoverage` is true when the active-version anchor is non-empty — a pipeline without a bootstrapped identity anchor is L5-silent even if rejections are being logged (a rejection log without an active identity is structurally meaningless). `distinctSubjectCount` unions the active, committed, pending, rejected, and frozen ID sets — a version that is both committed and frozen is one governance subject; the same candidate ID appearing in both pending and rejected (pathological but possible) collapses defensively. Updates the `BASCognitiveLayer` projection-status doc from 11-of-14 to 12-of-14 (remaining two: L2, L3). 10 new XCTests in `BASHostCandidatePipelineObservationCoverageTests`: `testProjectCoverageCarriesLayerAndFields`, `testPipelineWithoutActiveVersionHasNoCoreCoverage`, `testActiveVersionAloneIsCoreSignal`, `testFrozenVersionCollapsesWithCommittedVersion`, `testRejectionAndPendingCollapseOnSameCandidateID`, `testBudgetLinearlySumsComponents`, `testBudgetSkipsActiveComponentWhenEmpty`, `testBudgetClampsAtOne`, `testAsyncCoverageSummaryMatchesPurePath`, `testHostConstitutionSummaryFeedsReconciliationReport`. Regression: BAS 655 XCTest (+10) + 417 swift-testing green; Qinao SDK 162 XCTest green (1234 total green across both packages; 1 skipped). All four boundary + redaction scripts clean.

- **M39 — L1 lease-life coverage projection (additive)**: Extends the M32/M37/M38 projection wave from 10-of-14 to **11-of-14** layers. `BehavioralAISubstrate/Sources/BASLeaseLife/BASLeaseLifeObservationCoverage.swift` adds `BASLeaseLifeObservationBudget` (per-turn base cost 0.05 + 0.02 per cancelled breath, guard-level multiplier 1× for `.nominal`/`.watch`, 1.5× for `.throttle`, 2× for `.emergency`; clamped to [0, 1]) and an extension on `BASLeaseLifeCoordinator.TurnRecorded` providing `hasCoreSignalCoverage` (= `thermal.guardLevel != .emergency` — emergency means L1 spoke but did not sustain core function) and `coverageSummary(turnID:sessionID:)`. Like L8 (M37) and L14 (M38), the `TurnRecorded` value has no intrinsic turn/session — the caller pairs it with whatever turn/session context they're running in, so the projection takes keys as arguments. `totalObservations` is `2 + cancelledBreathIDs.count` (lung + thermal base observations plus one per scheduler cancel) and `distinctSubjectCount` is `2 + Set(cancelledBreathIDs).count` (defensive `Set` collapse against pathological duplicates). `emittedAt` uses `thermal.observedAt` as the closest wall-clock correspondence to when L1 produced the snapshot. Isolation preserved: the new file lives in `BASLeaseLife` and imports only `BASRuntimeCore`; `BASLeaseLifeCoordinator` / `BASLungStateAccumulator` / `BASThermalTwin` / `BASBreathScheduler` and every consumer path are untouched. Updates the `BASCognitiveLayer` projection-status doc from 10-of-14 to 11-of-14 (remaining three: L2, L3, L5). 12 new XCTests in `BASLeaseLifeObservationCoverageTests`: `testProjectionCarriesLayerAndFields`, `testNonEmergencyGuardLevelsHaveCoreSignalCoverage`, `testEmergencyGuardLevelLosesCoreSignalCoverage`, `testEachCancelledBreathAddsASubject`, `testDuplicateCancelledIDsCollapseInDistinctCount`, `testBudgetBaseCost`, `testBudgetAddsPerCancelledBreath`, `testBudgetMultiplierForThrottle`, `testBudgetMultiplierForEmergency`, `testBudgetClampsAtOne`, `testMultiplierTable`, `testLeaseLifeSummaryFeedsReconciliationReportAsEleventhLayer`. Regression: BAS 645 XCTest (+12) + 417 swift-testing green; Qinao SDK 162 XCTest green (1224 total green across both packages; 1 skipped). All four boundary + redaction scripts clean.

- **M38 — L14 sovereign audit-ledger coverage projection (additive)**: Extends the M32/M37 projection wave from 9-of-14 to **10-of-14** layers. `BehavioralAISubstrate/Sources/BASSovereign/BASSovereignAuditCoverage.swift` adds `BASSovereignAuditCoverageBudget` (per-actor entry cost: `.system` 0.10 / `.operator` 0.15) and two projection paths on `BASSovereignAuditLedger`: a pure `projectCoverage(from:turnID:sessionID:emittedAt:)` static helper for snapshot-in-hand callers and an async `coverageSummary(turnID:sessionID:emittedAt:)` that snapshots the chain then projects. `distinctSubjectCount` maps to distinct `verdictRef` strings in the scoped turn (two entries referencing the same verdict — verdict row + its token-issue row — collapse to one subject). `hasCoreSignalCoverage` requires at least one entry whose `ruleIDs` is non-empty: a bookkeeping entry (token rotation, snapshot boot check) proves the ledger is alive but not that the microkernel made a rule-bearing decision. The projection filters scope by turn AND session defensively so a multi-session ledger would still project correctly. Updates the `BASCognitiveLayer` projection-status doc from 9-of-14 to 10-of-14 (remaining four: L1, L2, L3, L5). 9 new XCTests in `BASSovereignAuditCoverageTests`: `testProjectCoverageCarriesLayerAndFields`, `testEmptyLedgerHasNoCoreSignalCoverage`, `testEntriesWithoutRuleIDsAreNotCoreSignal`, `testOperatorEntriesCostMoreThanSystemEntries`, `testBudgetClampsAtOne`, `testProjectionFiltersOutCrossTurnEntries`, `testProjectionFiltersOutCrossSessionEntries`, `testAsyncCoverageSummaryMatchesPurePath`, `testSovereignSummaryFeedsReconciliationReport`. Regression: BAS 633 XCTest (+9) + 417 swift-testing green; Qinao SDK 162 XCTest green. All four boundary + redaction scripts clean.

- **M37 — L8 tier reconciler coverage projection (additive)**: `BASMemoryTieringReconciliationOutcome` now projects into the neutral `BASObservationCoverageSummary` shape via `coverageSummary(turnID:sessionID:)` on `BehavioralAISubstrate/Sources/BASMemory/BASMemoryTieringObservationCoverage.swift`. Unlike the other eight layers whose observation bundles are turn-bound, a tier reconciliation sweep has no intrinsic turn/session — the governance coordinator binds it to an audit context by supplying the keys at projection time. `hasCoreSignalCoverage` is `evaluatedCount > 0` (an empty sweep is silent; a sweep with any decision — even all `.hold` — is a meaningful audit signal). New `BASMemoryTieringObservationBudget` tabulates per-transition L1 cost (`hold` 0.02 / `promote` 0.08 / `demote` 0.08 / `quarantineSuggest` 0.15 / `evictSuggest` 0.10) and clamps the total to [0, 1]. Updates the `BASCognitiveLayer` projection-status doc in `BASObservationReconciliationCore` from 8-of-14 to 9-of-14 (remaining five: L1, L2, L3, L5, L14). 4 new XCTests: `testTieringProjectionMapsAtomIDsAndBudget`, `testTieringProjectionEmptySweepReportsNoCoreCoverage`, `testTieringProjectionBudgetClampsAtOne`, `testTieringProjectionFeedsCrossLayerReportAsNinthLayer`. Regression: BAS 624 XCTest (+4) + 417 swift-testing green; Qinao SDK 162 XCTest green. All four boundary scripts clean.

- **M35 — M34 audit-trail closure (docs)**: Deep-check pass against the whitepaper / changelog / honesty-board surface found no CRITICAL or HIGH mismatches — matrix percentages (L1=85%, L4=75%, L5=95%, L11=85%, L13=70%, L14=92%) and all M30/M31/M32 file-path claims are backed by code in the repo. The one real gap was that M34's deep-test + fix commit landed without a changelog row; this row folds M34 into the 2026-04-22 block so the doc trail matches the commit graph (`e732d4a feat(M34): deep-test + fix audit findings on M30-M32 reconciliation`). Honesty Board entries keyed to M1–M8 remain intact; the M20–M34 observation-primitive wave is additive to those rows and does not revise any baseline mark.

- **M34 — deep-test + fix audit findings on M30–M32 reconciliation (additive)**: Closes the two CRITICAL invariant holes flagged by the post-M32 deep review of `BASObservationReconciliationCore`:
  1. **Codable bypassed dedup** — the synthesized `Decodable` conformance assigned the raw `[BASObservationCoverageSummary]` directly, letting crafted JSON smuggle duplicate layers past the dedup-on-layer invariant the designated init enforces. Fixed by adding a custom `init(from:)` that re-runs a shared `dedupedAndFiltered(_:turnID:sessionID:)` normalizer post-decode.
  2. **Mismatched turnID/sessionID silently retained** — the designated init and `appending(_:)` accepted summaries whose turn/session disagreed with the report. A coverage summary is turn-local by definition; cross-turn payloads are now dropped defensively on all three construction paths (init, appending, decode).

  Also documents the HIGH / MEDIUM findings: the L4 `hasCoreSignalCoverage` asymmetric `||` rationale (template match plus counterfactual OR bedrock is sufficient; requiring both would force every match to both branch-synthesize and touch bedrock), the 8-of-14 projection status directly on `BASCognitiveLayer` so readers know which layers carry bundle→summary coverage today and which are reserved for later waves, and the vacuous-truth semantics of `isFullyObserved(expected: [])`. 7 new XCTests pin the invariants: `testDecodingRejectsDuplicateLayersInPayload`, `testInitFiltersOutMismatchedTurnSummaries`, `testAppendingDropsMismatchedTurnSummary`, `testDecodingDropsMismatchedTurnSummaries`, `testTotalBudgetSaturatesAtEightProjectedLayers`, `testIsFullyObservedIsVacuouslyTrueForEmptyExpectation`, `testInitAndAppendedReportsAreEqualUnderSameInput`. Regression: BAS 620 XCTest (+7) + 417 swift-testing green; Qinao SDK 162 XCTest green.

- **M33 — M30/M31/M32 audit-trail alignment (docs)**: `docs/EBRAIN_13L_COMPLETION_MATRIX.md` now carries rows for M30/M31/M32, bumps L4 60%→75% and L14 90%→92%, and the shared-semantics paragraph is relabeled "M20–M32 共享语义" to describe how M31's neutral `BASObservationCoverageSummary` + `BASObservationReconciliationReport` in `BASRuntimeCore` plus M32's 8 per-layer `coverageSummary` edge projections close the curve that M22–M28 + M30 seeded: an L14 reconciler now reads one shape across L4/L6/L7/L9/L10/L11/L12/L13 instead of 8. This changelog block folds the same wave in.

- **M32 — bundle → coverage summary projections (additive)**: Each of the 8 observation-primitive bundles (`BASWorldPriorObservationBundle` / `BASPresenceObservationBundle` / `BASDecompositionObservationBundle` / `BASCandidateObservationBundle` / `BASTribunalObservationBundle` / `BASRiskObservationBundle` / `BASSoftHandObservationBundle` / `BASShadowTrialObservationBundle`) gets a `coverageSummary` edge projection that carries its `BASCognitiveLayer` tag implicitly and maps into the neutral `BASObservationCoverageSummary` shape from M31. Per-layer `distinctSubjectCount` mapping: L4→`templateIDs.count`, L6→distinct channels, L7→distinct kinds, L9→`candidateIDs.count`, L10→`subjectIDs.count` with `hasCoreSignalCoverage` projected from `allVoicesSpoke` (tribunal quorum health), L11→`intentIDs.count`, L12→`subjectIDs.count`, L13→`ticketIDs.count`. Each projection lives in its primitive's home module so `BASRuntimeCore` stays a leaf and `BASSovereign`'s isolation is preserved. 9 new XCTests including an end-to-end 8-layer reconciliation that builds one minimally-compliant bundle per layer, projects all 8, feeds them into a single `BASObservationReconciliationReport`, and asserts `isFullyObserved == true` with correct first-seen layer order.

- **M31 — cross-layer observation reconciliation scaffold (additive)**: `BASCognitiveLayer` enum (stable `L1`–`L14` raw values) + `BASObservationCoverageSummary` value (turnID / sessionID / totalObservations / distinctSubjectCount / hasCoreSignalCoverage / budgetTotalCost, defensively clamped on construction) + immutable `BASObservationReconciliationReport` aggregator keyed by turn+session with dedup-on-layer (last write wins, first-seen order preserved), `appending(_:)`, `summary(forLayer:)`, `coveredLayers`, `missingLayers(expected:)`, `layersWithoutCoreCoverage`, `totalObservations`, `totalBudgetCost` (sum-then-reclamp to [0,1]), and `isFullyObserved(expected:)` as the minimum bar before L14 declares a turn fully observed. All additive in `BASRuntimeCore`; zero upstream coupling — the file never imports an observation-producing module so the sovereign microkernel stays isolated. 17 new XCTests.

- **M30 — L4 world-prior observation primitives (additive)**: `BASWorldPriorSignalKind` (`templateMatched / counterfactualSeeded / boundaryBedrockConsulted / domainBridgeCrossed / evidenceRevised / priorContradiction`) plus `BASWorldPriorObservation` (carries `templateID` + `BASWorldPriorEvidenceLevel` so the uncertainty-grammar rung propagates into audit without re-opening the vault, clamped salience/confidence) and `BASWorldPriorObservationBundle` with `observations(of:)`, `observations(forTemplate:)`, first-seen `templateIDs`, `highestEvidenceLevel(forTemplate:)` using L4's own `Comparable` evidence ladder, `priorContradiction(forTemplate:)` heuristic, and `hasCoreSignalCoverage` (requires `templateMatched` plus either `counterfactualSeeded` or `boundaryBedrockConsulted` — proves the prior actually fired and produced reasoning or enforcement). `BASWorldPriorObservationBudget` makes `counterfactualSeeded` the priciest at 0.30 (branch synthesis against the vault prior). Ring-actor `BASWorldPriorObservationLedger`. All additive in `BASWorldPrior`; `BASWorldPriorVault` / `BASWorldPriorRiskAssessment` / `BASWorldPriorCounterfactualSeeder` and every consumer path untouched. 24 new XCTests. Closes the last major layer without observation primitives — the pattern now covers L4/L6/L7/L8/L9/L10/L11/L12/L13.

- **M29 — M20–M28 audit-trail alignment (docs)**: `docs/EBRAIN_13L_COMPLETION_MATRIX.md` now logs the M21–M28 observation-primitive wave row-by-row, bumps the key-layer map (L6 60% / L7 60% / L8 65% / L9 62% / L10 45% / L11 87% / L12 58% / L13 73%), and documents the shared additive-observation pattern (`SignalKind` enum → subject-addressable `Observation` → bundle with `observations(of:)` / `observations(for<Subject>:)` / `hasCoreSignalCoverage` / distinct first-seen subject IDs → per-kind `Budget` with clamped `totalCost` → append-only ring-actor `Ledger`) so any later L14 reconciler reads one shape across L6/L7/L9/L10/L11/L12/L13. This changelog block folds the same wave in.

- **M28 — L13 shadow-trial observation primitives (additive)**: `BASShadowTrialSignalKind` (`ticketIssued / trialRun / parityVerified / regressionDetected / promotionVote / quarantineVote`) plus `BASShadowTrialObservation` (carries `ticketID`, clamped salience/confidence) and `BASShadowTrialObservationBundle` with `observations(of:)`, `observations(forTicket:)`, first-seen `ticketIDs`, `hasCoreSignalCoverage` (requires `ticketIssued` + `trialRun` + (`parityVerified` or `regressionDetected`)), `netPromotionScore(forTicket:)` (promote − quarantine), and `regressionDetected(forTicket:)` heuristic. `BASShadowTrialObservationBudget` tabulates per-signal L1 cost with `trialRun` at 0.40 (sandboxed corpus pass is the most expensive signal) and clamps total to [0,1]. `BASShadowTrialObservationLedger` actor is a 128-default append-only ring with `record / snapshot / count / bundles(forSession:) / bundle(forTurn:) / clear`. All additive in `BASMemory`; no evolution coordinator path touched. 19 new XCTests.

- **M27 — L12 soft-hand observation primitives (additive)**: `BASSoftHandMode` (`compare / draft / delay / boundary / silentStub` — the five protection surfaces) and `BASSoftHandSignalKind` (`suggestion / selection / render / deferral / downgrade / escalation`) plus `BASSoftHandObservation` (carries `mode` + `subjectID`) and `BASSoftHandObservationBundle` with `observations(of:)`, `observations(forMode:)`, `observations(forSubject:)`, first-seen `subjectIDs`, `selectedMode` (latest selection), `renderedAsSelected` (proves the hand actually shipped the selected mode), and `hasCoreSignalCoverage` (requires `selection` + `render`). `BASSoftHandObservationBudget` makes `escalation` the priciest at 0.25 (stricter mode re-eval). Ring-actor `BASSoftHandObservationLedger`. All additive in `BASOrchestration`. 19 new XCTests.

- **M26 — L11 risk observation primitives (additive)**: `BASRiskSignalKind` (`hazardReading / irreversibilityReading / harmPotentialReading / consequenceHorizonReading / noveltyReading / gatePressure`) plus `BASRiskObservation` (carries `intentID`) and `BASRiskObservationBundle` with `observations(of:)`, `observations(forIntent:)`, first-seen `intentIDs`, `peakSalience(forIntent:)` (max across readings), and `hasCoreSignalCoverage` (requires the three pillars `hazard` + `irreversibility` + `harmPotential`). `BASRiskObservationBudget` makes `consequenceHorizonReading` the priciest at 0.30 (forward simulation). Ring-actor `BASRiskObservationLedger`. All additive in `BASPolicy`; risk gate evaluation logic untouched. 19 new XCTests.

- **M25 — L10 tribunal observation primitives (additive)**: `BASTribunalVoice` (`baseSelf 质我 / ruleSelf 律我 / aspireSelf 向我`), `BASTribunalDisposition` (`affirm / oppose / abstain`), and `BASTribunalSignalKind` (`vote / objection / concession / convergence / dissent`) plus `BASTribunalObservation` (nullable `voice` — convergence/dissent belong to the tribunal as a whole — and nullable `disposition` — only votes carry one) and `BASTribunalObservationBundle` with `observations(of:)`, `observations(by:)`, `observations(onSubject:)`, `allVoicesSpoke` quorum check, `vote(by:on:)` returning latest vote by voice × subject, `voicesAgree(on:)` (all three match), and `hasCoreSignalCoverage` (requires all three voices + at least one convergence/dissent). `BASTribunalObservationBudget` makes `objection` the priciest at 0.25. Ring-actor `BASTribunalObservationLedger`. All additive in `BASOrchestration`. 18 new XCTests.

- **M24 — L9 dream-loop candidate observation primitives (additive)**: `BASCandidateSignalKind` (`candidate / dominanceSignal / reversibilitySignal / guardianBranch / diversitySignal / delayRecommendation`) plus `BASCandidateObservation` (carries `candidateID`) and `BASCandidateObservationBundle` with `observations(of:)`, `observations(forCandidate:)`, first-seen `candidateIDs`, and `hasCoreSignalCoverage` (requires `candidate` + `reversibilitySignal` + `guardianBranch`). `BASCandidateObservationBudget` makes `guardianBranch` the priciest at 0.30 (counter-candidate synthesis). Ring-actor `BASCandidateObservationLedger`. All additive in `BASOrchestration`; dream-loop orchestration untouched. 17 new XCTests.

- **M23 — L7 mirror-blade decomposition observation primitives (additive)**: `BASDecompositionSignalKind` (`factShard / unknown / contradiction / pressure / manipulation / mirrorDraft`) plus `BASDecompositionObservation` and `BASDecompositionObservationBundle` with `observations(of:)`, `mirrorDrafts`, `pressureLoad`, and `hasCoreSignalCoverage` (requires `factShard` + `contradiction` + `mirrorDraft` — proves the blade actually cut and reflected). `BASDecompositionObservationBudget` makes `mirrorDraft` the priciest at 0.35 (draft synthesis). Ring-actor `BASDecompositionObservationLedger`. All additive in `BASOrchestration`. 16 new XCTests.

- **M22 — L6 presence-eye observation primitives (additive)**: `BASPresenceSignalKind` (`gaze / attentionShift / saliencePeak / salienceFade / contextTag / dwell`) plus `BASPresenceObservation` and `BASPresenceObservationBundle` with `observations(of:)`, first-seen `contextTags`, `peakSalience`, `hasCoreSignalCoverage` (requires `gaze` + `saliencePeak`), and `BASPresenceObservationBudget` with `saliencePeak` priciest at 0.20. Ring-actor `BASPresenceObservationLedger`. All additive in `BASOrchestration`. 16 new XCTests. This kicked off the uniform observation-primitive wave that M23–M28 extended to every remaining layer.

- **M21 — L8 tiering reconciler (additive observational pass)**: `BASMemoryTierTransitionReconciler` walks a set of `BASMemoryTieringProfile` inputs through `BASMemoryTemperaturePolicy.recommendTransition(_:)` and emits a `BASMemoryTierReconciliationReport` with per-atom transitions grouped by kind (hold / promote / demote / quarantineSuggest / evictSuggest). Pure `Sendable` pass — no `MemoryCore` write — so an L14 audit surface can later reconcile "what the reconciler observed" vs "what the promotion path actually did" without racing the hot path. 16 new XCTests covering empty input, single-branch paths, multi-atom mixed batches, quarantine precedence, eviction precedence, and stable output ordering.

- **M20 — L8 Temporal Memory Field Stage 1 primitives (additive)**: `BASMemoryTieringProfile` captures a memory atom's four-signal thermal profile (recency, access frequency, sensitivity drift, world-context staleness); `BASMemoryTemperaturePolicy.recommendTransition(_:)` is a pure `Sendable` function returning exactly one `BASMemoryTierTransition` (`hold / promote / demote / quarantineSuggest / evictSuggest`) with typed reasons; sensitivity escalation short-circuits world-context contamination, both short-circuit the tier ladder, and cold + moderately-stale suggests evict. `BASMemoryTierTransitionLog` is a ring-buffered actor (default 256 entries) that every reconciliation pass can append to so the L14 audit surface eventually reconciles against a tamper-evident trail. This commit is additive only — no `MemoryCore` promotion path changed — and lands 18 new unit tests covering clamping, the composite heat blend, every transition branch, quarantine precedence, eviction precedence, capacity, clear, and codable round-trip.

- **M9 — turn-audit parity contract**: The sovereign audit now carries a `parity` field (`match / coordinatorStricter / coordinatorLaxer / engineOnly`) so callers can tell whether the independent engine agreed, was stricter, or disagreed with the coordinator. Parity semantics are unit-tested end-to-end so subsequent milestones can key fail-closed logic off a typed field instead of free-form comparisons.

- **M10 — snapshot ark central registry + integrity check**: `BASSovereignSnapshotManager` becomes the single resolver for `safeSnapshotRef / foldRefs / hostVersionRef / integrityHash`; `BASSovereignHostVersionTree` exposes forward/backward/diff navigation; recovery paths verify the integrity hash on load and refuse resume on mismatch. Snapshot continuity proofs are no longer floating strings.

- **M11 — L5 host constitution pipeline + projection parity**: `BASHostCandidatePipeline` threads submit → preview → approve → reject → rollback through the coordinator; every mutation round-trips through the compatibility projection and a parity test proves the legacy host profile view is bit-equal with the typed host-version tree.

- **M12 — neural organ adapter + Apple Foundation Models**: `BASOrganAdapter` protocol plus a default provider that calls the on-device `FoundationModels` framework (iOS 18+). Scout/Core separation is expressed as distinct sampling profiles on the same adapter; local-MLX and other back-ends slot in without touching the substrate.

- **M13 — L11 risk consumes L4 WorldPrior**: `QinaoWorldPriorEndpoint` folds the matched template's `irreversibleHarmScore` into `signals.irreversibility` (max), forces `.replace` on unacknowledged informed-consent templates, forces `.delay` on low-evidence irreversible templates, and returns `RiskError.unknownWorldTemplate` for taxonomy misses. Ten new tests cover every branch.

- **M14 — offline distillation triple gate**: `QinaoLearningExportBundle` exposes `scrubbed + privacySafe + sovereignSafe` — all three must be green before an export leaves the device. Host-private host-constitution fields, L8 memory sensitivity tiers, and the audit ledger's clearance are each independent gates; any missing one refuses the export with a typed `QinaoLearningExportError`. 14 new tests.

- **M15 — `sendSession` main-path turn audit**: Every turn — not just side-effect execution — now flows through an independent sovereign audit. Pre-flight refuses a halted session (`TurnError.sessionAlreadyHalted`); parity `.coordinatorLaxer` halts with reason `audit-parity:coordinator-laxer` then throws `TurnError.auditParityFailure`; severity `.rollback` / `.deadStop` halt with reason `audit-severity:<level>` and return `sessionHalted: true`; `.quarantine` flows through so the host decides. Five new tests.

- **M16 — Apple BGTaskScheduler bridge**: `QinaoBGMaintenanceBridge` realises `BASBreathScheduler.PlatformBridge` against `BGTaskScheduler.shared` on iOS 13+ / tvOS 13+ / visionOS 1+ / Mac Catalyst 13.1+; macOS and watchOS get a no-op fallback so the scheduler still tracks the breath. The substrate stays a leaf module (no `BackgroundTasks` import). Eight new tests exercise the identifier protocol + end-to-end round-trip against the real scheduler actor.
