- Promoted the Session Engine `pending import` digest into the control-center header as part of the same shared review surface: [`DecisionSessionEnginePendingImportBlockView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/Components/DecisionSessionEnginePendingImportBlockView.swift) now wraps the shared import-preview digest, [`DecisionSessionEnginePanelView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/Components/DecisionSessionEnginePanelView.swift) and [`DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView.swift) both consume it, and [`DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot.swift) now carries the pending import preview directly so the control center no longer shares only the digest headline while hiding the import draft detail block.
- Made the Session Engine control center consume the same shared `review digest` contract as the folded-lung panel and import/merge review surfaces: [`DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot.swift) now synthesizes `Pending import draft` / `Merge review queue` / `Replay anchor ready` items directly from runtime + per-session facts, using the stronger of aggregate snapshot counts and derived session-level merge facts so partial runtime snapshots no longer hide merge review pressure.
- Added a shared Session Engine `review digest` contract on top of the folded-lung runtime summary: [`DecisionSessionEnginePresentation`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEnginePresentation.swift) now derives unified `Pending import draft` / `Merge review queue` / `Replay anchor ready` items, [`DecisionSessionEnginePanelView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/Components/DecisionSessionEnginePanelView.swift) renders that digest across `Home / History / Portrait / Settings`, and [`DecisionSystemFlightDeck`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSystemFlightDeck.swift) now forwards the full non-headline Session Engine signal set into the data layer so per-session checkpoint and merge-review facts no longer disappear from runtime export.
- Added a service-level [`DecisionSessionEngineReviewDigestBuilder`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngineReviewDigestBuilder.swift) and routed folded-lung flight-deck review lines through it, so `pending import / merge review / replay anchor` data-layer signals no longer depend on the UI presentation builder to reach runtime-export consumers.
- Extended folded-lung flight-deck signals with structured replay/recovery lines derived from [`DecisionSessionEngineReplayRecoveryPresentation`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngineReplayRecoveryPresentation.swift), so runtime-export consumers now see `Checkpoint recovery / Replay session / stable checkpoint` facts even outside Self Portrait and replay-specific views.
- Added a dedicated [`DecisionSessionEngineHealthSummaryTests.swift`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/DecisionSessionEngineHealthSummaryTests.swift) matrix, pinning empty / watchdog-overdue / recovered / stalled summary behavior directly at the shared health-summary layer instead of only through higher-level view regressions.
- Promoted `merge-ready` correction branches into the shared Session Engine runtime contract: [`DecisionSessionEngine`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngine.swift) now records per-session `mergeableBranchCount` inside runtime inspection, [`DecisionSessionEnginePresentation`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEnginePresentation.swift) and [`DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot.swift) surface that as explicit merge-review copy, [`DecisionSessionEnginePanelView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/Components/DecisionSessionEnginePanelView.swift) and [`DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView.swift) now render it directly, and [`DecisionSystemFlightDeck`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSystemFlightDeck.swift) carries the same signal into folded-lung runtime reports so correction branches waiting to merge back onto head are visible outside the dedicated branch inspector.
- Replaced scattered Session Engine watchdog/recovery strings with a shared structured health summary: [`DecisionSessionEnginePresentation`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEnginePresentation.swift) now derives severity-scoped `DecisionSessionEngineHealthSummary` objects for global runtime state and per-session state, [`DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot.swift) carries the same summary into control-center inventory, and the shared [`DecisionSessionEngineHealthSummaryView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/Components/DecisionSessionEngineHealthSummaryView.swift) now renders that contract in both [`DecisionSessionEnginePanelView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/Components/DecisionSessionEnginePanelView.swift) and [`DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView.swift), so `Home / History / Portrait / Settings / Control Center` no longer drift on watchdog vs recovery vs stable-session copy.
- Made folded Session Engine merges visible again after checkpoint compaction: [`DecisionSessionEngine`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngine.swift) now emits a structured `mergeNotice` when the latest checkpoint has already folded `branch_merged` facts out of the visible post-checkpoint event list, [`DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot.swift) carries that notice into the selected-session summary, and [`DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView.swift) renders it beside recovery state so branch merges no longer disappear just because a new stable checkpoint was created afterwards.
- Promoted Session Engine bundle import into a host-owned staged flow: [`BeforeAppModel`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/BeforeAppModel.swift) now keeps a `pendingSessionEngineImportDraft` plus bundle issue state, [`DecisionSessionEngine`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngine.swift) can inspect/import directly from in-memory bundle data, and both [`DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView.swift) and [`DecisionSessionEngineActionBarView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/Components/DecisionSessionEngineActionBarView.swift) now drive the same staged preview/import path instead of each re-reading the selected file independently.
- Hardened Session Engine bundle export/import with explicit integrity checks: [`DecisionSessionEngine`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngine.swift) now exports a stable bundle fingerprint, validates schema/head-branch/latest-checkpoint/count/reference integrity before both preview and import, and surfaces a validation line in [`DecisionSessionEngineImportPreviewSheetView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/Components/DecisionSessionEngineImportPreviewSheetView.swift) so the user sees that the bundle passed validation before committing it into the local store.
- Raised Session Engine fault/recovery visibility in the shared control surface: [`DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot.swift) now derives explicit `faultLine` state for stalled/watchdog-overdue/recovered sessions and tags derived branches as `Correction branch` / `Recovery branch`, while [`DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView.swift) renders those summaries directly in the session inventory and selected-session card instead of hiding them only inside timeline rows.
- Extended the host-owned Session Engine tool lifecycle path in [`BeforeAppModel`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/BeforeAppModel.swift) so real Quick / Balance / Mirror actions now create a dedicated `waiting_tool -> writing -> completed/stalled` step with heartbeats around `tool_call_started` / `tool_call_finished`, instead of only emitting tool events without watchdog-visible step state.
- Expanded [`BeforeAppModelSessionEngineTests.swift`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/BeforeAppModelSessionEngineTests.swift) to pin the new tool-lifecycle step semantics, including dedicated completed tool steps, heartbeat presence, and higher-level step-start / heartbeat event counts after host actions.
- Surfaced Session Engine heartbeat freshness and watchdog pressure across the shared runtime surfaces: [`DecisionSessionEngine`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngine.swift) now carries open-step heartbeat/TTL inspection into runtime snapshots, [`DecisionSessionEnginePresentation`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEnginePresentation.swift) and [`DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot.swift) translate that into recoverability and warning copy, [`DecisionSessionEnginePanelView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/Components/DecisionSessionEnginePanelView.swift) and [`DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView.swift) render the new freshness/alert lines, and [`DecisionSystemFlightDeck`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSystemFlightDeck.swift) now includes watchdog-budget signals in the Session Engine summary.
- Expanded regression coverage in [`DecisionSessionEnginePresentationTests.swift`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/DecisionSessionEnginePresentationTests.swift) and [`DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshotTests.swift`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshotTests.swift), then re-ran targeted Session Engine plus runtime-export suites so heartbeat-overdue recovery affordances stay pinned across host panels, control center inventory, app-model integration, and testing/runtime export bridges.
- Completed the first host-exposed Session Engine checkpoint-restore path: [`DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot.swift) now marks restore-capable checkpoint timeline rows, [`DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView.swift) can restore directly from those rows, and [`BeforeAppModel`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/BeforeAppModel.swift) bridges the action back into `DecisionSessionEngine.restoreFromCheckpoint(...)`, creating a recovery branch without rewriting the original branch head history.
- Added restore-focused regressions in [`DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshotTests.swift`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshotTests.swift) and [`BeforeAppModelSessionEngineTests.swift`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/BeforeAppModelSessionEngineTests.swift), locking checkpoint row restore affordances plus the host-visible “restore creates recovery branch and moves head” path.
- Expanded [`BeforeAppModelSessionEngineTests.swift`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/BeforeAppModelSessionEngineTests.swift) with host-surface recovery and watchdog coverage, pinning the startup notices for `recoverSessionEngineSession(...)` and `sweepSessionEngineWatchdog()` while verifying that stalled-step recovery actually advances the session head onto a recovered branch.
- Extended the local [`DecisionSessionEngine`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngine.swift) integration beyond evaluation turns so real Quick / Balance / Mirror product actions now append `tool_call_started` / `tool_call_finished` lifecycle events plus action-enriched checkpoints from [`BeforeAppModel`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/BeforeAppModel.swift), making saved boards, tomorrow moves, and completed quick checks recoverable from the immutable L3 session log instead of only from SwiftData artifacts.
- Hardened [`NotificationService`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/NotificationService.swift) for XCTest runs by short-circuiting notification authorization and scheduling under test, so Session Engine action recovery tests do not hang on system notification prompts while preserving production behavior on-device.
- Added host-owned correction branching from the Session Engine control surface: [`BeforeAppModel`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/BeforeAppModel.swift) can now resolve a stable target event on the current head branch and append a `correction_added` event that automatically fans out into a new branch, while [`DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView.swift) exposes the flow as “Create correction branch” instead of rewriting old history.
- Bound live Quick / Balance / Mirror evaluations to the local [`DecisionSessionEngine`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngine.swift) from [`BeforeAppModel`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/BeforeAppModel.swift), so primary product turns now append user/assistant events, start and complete steps, and auto-attach checkpoint drafts instead of leaving the L3 folded-lung layer idle during real work.
- Added active-session binding helpers in [`BeforeAppModel`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/BeforeAppModel.swift) so restored or newly opened Quick / Balance / Mirror workspaces get stable Session Engine session IDs before evaluation, and failures now degrade into explicit startup notices instead of silent history gaps.
- Surfaced the shared [`DecisionSessionEnginePresentation`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEnginePresentation.swift) inside [`HistoryView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/HistoryView.swift), so the Evolution trail now shows live Session Engine checkpoint/recovery state and links straight into the Session Engine control center.
- Added [`BeforeAppModelSessionEngineTests.swift`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/BeforeAppModelSessionEngineTests.swift) to lock Quick / Balance runtime event writing, checkpoint creation, and Session Engine visibility through the flight-deck path after mirror evaluation.

- Added a dedicated [`DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView.swift) with host-owned pause/resume/recover/archive controls, watchdog sweep, and timeline-rebuild inspection so the L3 folded-lung session layer now has an operational recovery surface.
- Added shared [`DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot.swift) plus new `BeforeAppModel` Session Engine control APIs, so session inventory, selected-checkpoint metadata, and recovery timelines come from one reusable contract instead of ad-hoc page logic.
- Wired Session Engine control-center entry points into [`HomeView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/HomeView.swift), [`SelfPortraitView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/SelfPortraitView.swift), [`SettingsView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/SettingsView.swift), and [`RootTabView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/RootTabView.swift), then added regression coverage in [`DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshotTests.swift`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshotTests.swift).

- Added a shared [`DecisionSessionEnginePresentation`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEnginePresentation.swift) plus [`DecisionSessionEnginePanelView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/Components/DecisionSessionEnginePanelView.swift), so Session Engine recovery facts no longer live only in flight-deck signals and can render through one product-owned presentation contract.
- Surfaced the new Session Engine panel in [`HomeView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/HomeView.swift), [`SelfPortraitView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/SelfPortraitView.swift), and [`SettingsView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/SettingsView.swift), making the folded-lung branch/checkpoint/recovery line visible outside diagnostics.
- Added presentation regressions in [`DecisionSessionEnginePresentationTests.swift`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/DecisionSessionEnginePresentationTests.swift) to lock active-session vs recent-session rendering and the no-summary empty state.

- Expanded [`DecisionSessionEngine`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSessionEngine.swift) from a count-only runtime snapshot into a richer inspection surface that now carries recent sessions, active branch/checkpoint facts, latest event type/detail, open-step state, and recovery timestamps.
- Added `listSessions(limit:)` plus per-session inspection synthesis in the session engine, so the folded-lung layer can expose recent recovery state without replaying the whole timeline from the product shell.
- Updated [`DecisionSystemFlightDeck`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSystemFlightDeck.swift) to surface recent Session Engine inspection lines, including latest checkpoint/recovery state for the most recent sessions instead of only global counts.
- Added regression coverage in [`DecisionSessionEngineTests`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/DecisionSessionEngineTests.swift) and [`DecisionTestingInterfaceTests`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/DecisionTestingInterfaceTests.swift) to lock the richer folded-lung inspection snapshot through both the engine and flight-deck export path.

- Flipped the host-default assistive provider from `Gemma 4 E4B` to `Apple Foundation Model` in [`BeforePreferences.default`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/BeforePreferencesStore.swift), while keeping Gemma, the reserved open-model slot, and deterministic template mode user-selectable.
- Expanded [`SettingsView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/SettingsView.swift) so the host now exposes an explicit preferred-provider picker, fallback toggle, active-provider status, and per-provider availability rows instead of hiding provider choice behind the runtime summary.
- Clarified Gemma copy to treat previously downloaded or bundled `Gemma 4 E4B` assets as an optional local runtime that users can switch back to after Apple Foundation Models becomes the default lane.
- Added a local Gemma library flow: [`GemmaModelAssetCatalog`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/GemmaModelAssetCatalog.swift) now supports imported `.litertlm` assets under the app's local storage, prefers complete imported assets before bundled fallbacks, and exposes remove/import helpers for host-owned management.
- Updated [`GemmaLocalRuntimeBridge`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/GemmaLocalRuntimeBridge.swift) and [`GemmaE4BIntelligenceService`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/GemmaE4BIntelligenceService.swift) so Gemma inference resolves against the preferred local asset instead of only the app bundle.
- Expanded [`SettingsView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/SettingsView.swift) with a real Gemma library surface: users can import a previously-downloaded `.litertlm` file from Files, see imported vs bundled assets, identify the preferred asset, and remove imported assets without touching bundled fallbacks.
- Persisted an explicit `preferredGemmaAssetID` inside [`BeforePreferences`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/BeforePreferencesStore.swift), so hosts can pin a specific imported or bundled Gemma runtime instead of relying only on automatic ordering.
- Gave [`GemmaModelAsset`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/GemmaModelAssetCatalog.swift) a stable `assetID` (`source:fileName`) and switched Settings/runtime selection to that identity, preventing bundled and imported assets with the same filename from collapsing into one ambiguous choice.
- Extended [`BeforeAppModel`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/BeforeAppModel.swift) and [`SettingsView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/SettingsView.swift) so importing a Gemma file automatically pins it, users can later return to automatic selection, and selection changes emit host-visible startup notices instead of silently mutating runtime state.
- Added a host-owned [`GemmaModelDownloadService`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/GemmaModelDownloadService.swift), so Before can download a direct `.litertlm` URL into the local Gemma library instead of only importing files that already exist in Files.
- Expanded [`SettingsView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/SettingsView.swift) with a `Download Gemma from URL` sheet, including direct-link validation, in-progress download state, and automatic pinning once the downloaded asset lands in the local library.
- Added an explicit open-model runtime status surface to [`BeforeAppModel`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/BeforeAppModel.swift) so Settings can present Apple / Gemma / open-model availability from one host-owned contract.
- Extended [`DecisionTestingRuntimeSnapshot`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionTestingInterface.swift) with the open-model provider status plus the registered provider catalog, making runtime diagnostics and host settings consume the same registry-backed adapter facts.
- Expanded [`SettingsView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/SettingsView.swift) with a registry-backed `Registered model adapters` surface, so Apple, Gemma, and the reserved open-model slot show their real adapter track plus open-model stable IDs instead of only appearing as preference labels.
- Extended the Settings-side provider catalog with capability-profile summaries, so each registered adapter now explains latency, memory, thinking/tool support, response languages, and best-fit tasks in host-readable terms.
- Converted preferred-provider and fallback toggles into host-owned actions on [`BeforeAppModel`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/BeforeAppModel.swift), so changing Apple / Gemma / open-model preference or enabling/disabling fallback now emits startup notices instead of silently mutating settings state.
- Added a shared [`DecisionLocalModelLibrarySnapshot`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionLocalModelLibrarySnapshot.swift), moving local-model support facts out of `SettingsView` and into a reusable service layer that can describe today's Gemma flow alongside the reserved open-model slot.
- Wired the shared local-model library facts into [`DecisionSystemFlightDeck`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Services/DecisionSystemFlightDeck.swift) and the runtime-summary compatibility layer so diagnostics now surface Apple-default / Gemma-asset / open-model-slot state outside Settings.
- Surfaced the same local-model library summary in [`SelfPortraitView`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/App/Views/SelfPortraitView.swift), keeping Portrait aligned with Settings and the flight deck.
- Renamed the Settings-side Gemma section to `Local model library`, surfaced the open-model slot summary there, and framed Gemma 4 E4B as the current downloadable local-model format instead of implying Gemma is the whole model-management story forever.
- Updated default-preference regression coverage in [`BeforePreferencesStoreTests`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/BeforePreferencesStoreTests.swift) so missing legacy provider state now migrates to `Apple Foundation Model` instead of `Gemma 4 E4B`.
- Added Gemma catalog regression coverage for import, removal, imported-vs-bundled preference ordering, and explicit asset identity in [`GemmaModelAssetCatalogTests`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/GemmaModelAssetCatalogTests.swift).
- Added download-path regression coverage for URL validation, HTTP failure handling, and successful `.litertlm` imports in [`GemmaModelDownloadServiceTests`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/GemmaModelDownloadServiceTests.swift).
- Added provider-catalog and provider-notice regressions in [`DecisionTestingInterfaceTests`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/DecisionTestingInterfaceTests.swift) and [`BeforeAppModelProviderSelectionTests.swift`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/BeforeAppModelProviderSelectionTests.swift).
- Added `DecisionLocalModelLibrarySnapshotTests` to lock the shared local-model snapshot to both Gemma assets and the open-model slot summary.
- Expanded `DecisionTestingInterfaceTests` and `DecisionTestingRuntimeExportCompatibility` so runtime export, flight deck, and compatibility summaries all agree on local-model library facts.

- `Apple Foundation Model` is now the default host preference, while `Gemma 4 E4B`, the generic `open model runtime`, and deterministic fallback remain selectable in the host settings surface.
- Added a local `OpenModelAssetCatalog` plus `OpenModelDownloadService`, so previously downloaded `.gguf` / `.onnx` / `.safetensors` / `.litertlm` assets can be imported into the host-owned open-model library instead of living only as a reserved slot.
- The built-in open-model provider now supports a safe `ConfiguredOpenModelPreviewAdapter` path: when a preferred imported open-model asset exists, the registry exposes that asset as the active open-model descriptor while still falling back to the reserved slot when the library is empty.
- The configurable open-model preview bridge now reuses the same safe `TemplateLocalModelAdapter` heuristics as the deterministic local fallback, so imported open-model assets can already enhance `quick / balance / mirror / reminder` flows instead of acting like a pure pass-through placeholder.
- `DecisionLocalModelLibrarySnapshot`, its shared presentation layer, and the shared local-model library panel now surface both Gemma assets and generic open-model assets, including explicit preferred-asset selection, import, download, and removal controls from `Settings`.
- `BeforeAppModel`, runtime snapshots, and the flight deck now publish open-model library facts alongside Gemma facts, so `Home / Settings / Portrait / runtime export` all read the same local-model library truth source.
- Added host-level regressions for the open-model library flow in [`BeforeAppModelProviderSelectionTests`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/BeforeAppModelProviderSelectionTests.swift), covering `import -> prefer -> automatic fallback -> remove -> reserved-slot restore` so the host cannot silently drift away from the provider registry contract.
- Added runtime-export coverage in [`DecisionTestingInterfaceTests`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BeforeTests/DecisionTestingInterfaceTests.swift) to prove an imported preferred open-model asset survives all the way from `DecisionTestingInterface.runtimeSnapshot(...)` into `runtimeExport(...)` and the folded-lung flight-deck local-model summary.
## 2026-04-11

### Added

- Introduced `BASHostKit` as the façade-first private SDK product.
- Added `BASHostConfiguration`, `BASHostDependencySet`, `BASHostRuntime`, `BASHostSessionRequest`, `BASHostSessionResult`, `BASHostLifecycleRequest`, `BASHostReopenRequest`, and `BASHostConsoleConfiguration`.
- Added `SampleHost` as a minimal iOS façade integration example.
- Added package tests covering façade bootstrap, session start, and reopen flows.
- Added `check_sdk_import_boundaries.sh` to keep host targets on the façade import surface.

### Changed

- `Before`, `BeforeWatch`, and shared host source now import `BASHostKit` instead of low-level BAS modules.
- `project.yml` now treats `BASHostKit` as the primary host-facing dependency.
- `BehavioralAISubstrate` package structure is now documented as a private SDK delivery shape rather than only an internal substrate.
- `BASHostKit` façade now prefers generic host vocabulary such as `interactive`, `ambient`, `primary`, `comparative`, and `reflective` instead of exposing `Before` product language at the SDK boundary.
- Structured truth, current-brain mode identifiers, and provider observation narratives now prefer substrate-owned generic identifiers such as `primary`, `comparative`, and `reflective`.
- Reserved open-model slot identifiers are now substrate-owned (`substrate/open-model-slot`) instead of carrying `Before` branding.
- Host preview labels and other product phrasing now live in `Before` mappings instead of package-owned preview formatters.
- Provider runtime narration now refers to the host app generically instead of naming `Before`.
- Entry-intent, pending-launch, and deferred-reopen package surfaces now prefer generic workflow vocabulary like `capture`, `present`, `reopen`, `resume`, and `routedInput`, while legacy host raw values remain accepted at the bridge edge.
- `BASHostKit` now exposes host-owned presentation configuration so app-specific workflow titles, session titles, and follow-up phrasing can live in the host instead of the substrate default copy.
- `BASHostKit` host-owned presentation now also covers lifecycle notices, predictive intervention copy, reopen wording, and empty-prompt fallback copy, so substrate defaults stay generic while hosts own visible tone.
- `BASHostKit` now also exposes host-owned workflow behavior configuration, so template IDs, retrieval defaults, memory-source mapping, and verification/provenance namespace can live in each host instead of staying frozen inside substrate defaults.
- `Before` now injects its own `Quick Judgment / Balance Board / Mirror` presentation profile through `BASHostConfiguration.presentation`, while `SampleHost` continues to use the generic façade defaults.
- `Before` now also injects its own workflow behavior namespace and template IDs through `BASHostConfiguration.workflowBehavior`, while `SampleHost` proves a second host can carry a different namespace and workflow template set without forking SDK logic.
- `SampleHost` now proves that a second host can inject a totally different presentation profile (`Rapid Lens / Compare Lens / Reflective Lens`) without forking substrate logic.
- Identity role titles in the substrate default profile are now more generic (`Stability Guide`, `Comparative Guide`, `Reflective Witness`, `Risk Sentinel`) so persona flavor can be layered back in the host instead of leaking from the SDK core.
- Reference prompt slot vocabulary is now substrate-generic by default. Package-owned prompt builders now emit neutral state keys and evidence labels such as `entry_context`, `current_drive`, `Present view`, and `Priority label` unless a host injects its own slot vocabulary.
- `Before` now explicitly injects its legacy prompt slot vocabulary (`scenario`, `motivation`, `Current perspective`, `Focus title`, `Core tension`, and related labels) back through host configuration instead of relying on substrate defaults.
- Reference prompt request/builder façade now prefers substrate-generic types and names such as `BASPrimaryRefinementPromptRequest`, `BASComparativeRefinementPromptRequest`, `BASReflectiveRefinementPromptRequest`, `BASSelectionPromptRequest`, `primaryEnvelope`, `comparativeEnvelope`, `reflectiveEnvelope`, and `selectionEnvelope`.
### Notes

- The contract is intentionally fast-evolving and private. Breaking changes are allowed, but they must be recorded here and in the migration notes.
# 2026-04-12

- Generalized bootstrap risk defaults so `BehavioralAISubstrate` no longer encodes mode-specific daytime or night fallback risk levels in `BASBrainBootstrapAdvisorBehavior.generic`.
- Kept product-specific bootstrap risk heuristics in host-owned configuration, preserving `Before` and `SampleHost` as distinct products on top of a more neutral substrate.
- Shifted current-brain bootstrap fallback ownership to hosts by adding host-configurable default mode, trigger, and risk fallback policy to the substrate bootstrap behavior.
- Added regression coverage for unknown host bridge identifiers and a foreign-vocabulary host fixture so hidden `Before` worldview leakage is easier to catch.
- Hard-cut the substrate surface to generic identifiers only. Legacy `Before` request names, entry vocabulary, memory-source vocabulary, and prompt helper aliases no longer live in `BehavioralAISubstrate/Sources`.
- Moved legacy compatibility ownership into the `Before` host through [`BeforeProductCompatibility`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/Shared/Domain/BeforeProductCompatibility.swift) and [`BeforeLegacyMigration`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/Shared/Domain/BeforeLegacyMigration.swift).
- Added `check_substrate_residuals.sh` and wired it into `check_sdk_import_boundaries.sh` plus all quality gates, so `Before` vocabulary cannot quietly leak back into substrate source.
- Expanded `check_substrate_residuals.sh` to scan the substrate README and non-whitelisted package tests, so generic public teaching surfaces and fixtures stay clean too.
- Moved execution-profile thresholds and product-facing execution narration out of substrate assumptions and into host-owned behavior, while keeping the substrate adapter generic.
- Moved memory-derivation phrasing for concise-language and late-session patterns into host-owned derivation behavior, so substrate now detects patterns while hosts own the interpretation copy.
- Centralized `Before` prompt, predictive-intervention, execution-profile, and memory-derivation semantics in [`BeforeProductCompatibility`](/Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/Before/Shared/Domain/BeforeProductCompatibility.swift), so `Before/App/Services` consumes host compatibility instead of reaching into product language directly.
- Rewrote generic package fixtures and prompt/runtime tests to use substrate vocabulary (`primary / comparative / reflective / archive / selection`) rather than `Before` lane names, while leaving explicit legacy-rejection coverage in dedicated tests only.
