- Tightened `DecisionEvolutionPilotControlSnapshot` around resolved release facts, so pending-review count, rollback-ready count, active-kill-switch posture, restore readiness, and guided-action routing now flow from one snapshot-level resolution path instead of mixing raw control-surface checks with summary-only branches.
- Added `DecisionEvolutionControlSurface.activeKillSwitches`, making the active-checkpoint kill-switch posture a first-class control-surface fact instead of forcing downstream snapshots to re-open the active checkpoint directly.
- Added focused view-level evolution navigation coverage through `DecisionEvolutionNavigationActionRowTests`, locking the shared control-center / history / portrait shortcut row to the same destination-precedence contract already enforced in the surface-contract tests.
- Expanded `DecisionEvolutionPilotControlSnapshotTests` to cover the guided-action matrix across mutation-hub and read-first surfaces, including history / portrait / control-center routing and the no-summary fallback path.
- Added a shared `DecisionEvolutionOperatorActionFooterView` and moved release-summary, checkpoint-action, pilot-control, and read-first kill-switch navigation guidance onto it, so the operator headline/detail plus control-center/history/portrait shortcut strip now comes from one component instead of four drifting copies.
- Added `DecisionEvolutionRuntimeFacts` to `DecisionTestingRuntimeExport` and routed coverage / flight-deck / console consumers through that canonical bundle, reducing repeated derivation of recovered checkpoint coverage, effective kill-switch posture, audit findings, ticket summaries, and effective eBrain source.
- Added a parity regression for `DecisionTestingRuntimeExport.evolutionRuntimeFacts(...)`, locking the new canonical bundle to the existing export accessors while the service layer continues converging on one evolution-facts source of truth.
- Added shared `DecisionEvolutionWorkspaceFacts`, so workspace-level pending review, rollback readiness, restore readiness, and kill-switch posture now resolve once before feeding operator snapshots, attention signals, widget rollout state, and downstream release-facing surfaces.
- Moved `DecisionEvolutionOperatorSnapshot` onto shared workspace facts plus a shared operator-guidance derivation, reducing duplicated fallback logic for read-first vs mutation-hub headlines and primary reasons.
- Added `DecisionEvolutionReleaseSummaryPresentation`, so `DecisionEvolutionReleaseSummaryView` now renders active/review checkpoint copy, active-source text, kill-switch text, and mutation-hub operator copy from one presentation builder instead of rebuilding those strings inline.
- Added shared `DecisionEvolutionRuntimeSpotlight`, so Home's 13-layer runtime card now reads active/review checkpoint role, active-source detail, and apply-readiness copy from the workspace projection instead of rebuilding that spotlight line locally.
- Added shared replay diagnostics/source-descriptor presentation in `DeveloperDecisionReplayBuilder`, so live turns, replay entries, checkpoint lineage, and Self Portrait now render source, audit, kill-switch, and trace detail from one contract instead of rebuilding those strings per card.
- Moved `SettingsView` onto the shared evolution navigation contract, replacing local control/portrait/history buttons with the common `DecisionEvolutionNavigationActionRow` so read-first navigation and mutation-hub routing now stay in lockstep with the rest of the control surfaces.
- Added focused replay diagnostics regressions in `DeveloperDecisionReplayBuilderTests`, covering shared source descriptors plus live-turn and replay-entry diagnostics presentation so provider-title or checkpoint-source drift gets caught close to the service layer.
- Added shared `DecisionSystemEBrainSummaryPresentation` plus workspace-level `DecisionEvolutionRecoveryPresentation`, so `SelfPortraitView` now consumes shared runtime/recovery copy for the top-level 13-layer status card and checkpoint-recovery empty states instead of assembling those lines locally.
- Expanded workspace/replay regressions to cover the new shared runtime-summary and recovery presentation helpers, keeping live-runtime vs checkpoint-recovery wording pinned at the service layer instead of only in the views.
- Added `DecisionSessionEngine` as a local Session Engine v1 skeleton under the L3 folded-lung runtime boundary, backed by `SQLite + WAL` with append-only events, checkpoints, correction branches, watchdog recovery, and rebuildable timelines; clarified its 13-layer placement in the execution docs and completion matrix.
- Attached the L3 `DecisionSessionEngine` runtime snapshot to `DecisionTestingRuntimeExport` and `DecisionSystemFlightDeck`, so folded-lung session health now surfaces through the main runtime export path and contributes explicit runtime/data signals instead of living only as a standalone service.
- Promoted Session Engine branch management into the host control surface: the control snapshot now exposes explicit branch inventory with head/switch/abandon semantics plus non-head timeline inspection, `BeforeAppModel` owns branch switching and safe abandonment notices, and the control center can move between version lines without rewriting append-only history.
- Added a shared `DecisionSessionEngineResultActionsCard`, wiring Quick, Balance, and Mirror result surfaces directly into Session Engine so users can open the control center or create a correction branch from the current result without leaving the active workspace path.
- Extended the shared Session Engine action card into the live Quick / Balance / Mirror workspaces, so the control center and correction-branch flow are available before a result is committed instead of only after evaluation.
- Completed the first host-exposed Session Engine merge path: active correction branches can now be merged back into the current head branch from the control center, merge is recorded as an append-only `branch_merged` event on the target branch, merged branches become non-switchable inventory entries, and merge coverage now verifies raw event persistence plus rebuilt-timeline visibility without relying on a post-merge auto-checkpoint to hide the merge fact.
- Completed Session Engine v1 bundle portability: host actions can now export a session into a local JSON bundle, import it back as a paused recovery-safe session, and normalize unfinished imported steps into explicit failed recovery facts instead of reviving half-closed work.
- Promoted Session Engine bundle controls into the shared product shells: Home, History, Self Portrait, and Settings now all use one `DecisionSessionEngineActionBarView` for `open / import / export / share`, so recovery-line portability no longer requires drilling into the dedicated control center first.
- Moved Quick / Balance / Mirror Session Engine result cards onto the same shared action bar, so result/workspace recovery surfaces now inherit the same `open / import / export / share` contract instead of keeping a separate open-only control path.
- Routed `DecisionTestingEBrainSource` titles through the shared source-descriptor contract and moved Home's guardrail label onto the same shared eBrain presentation, trimming two more UI-facing string forks from the runtime surfaces.
- Added shared `DecisionLocalModelLibraryPresentation` plus `DecisionLocalModelLibraryPanelView`, so Settings and Self Portrait now render the same Gemma/open-model library facts, preferred-asset detail, asset readiness copy, and empty-state messaging instead of hand-building parallel local-model panels.
- Extended local-model regressions to cover the shared presentation builder, locking preferred Gemma selection text, removable-vs-bundled asset posture, and the no-asset empty state before more local runtimes land behind the same library surface.
- Added Session Engine preflight review before mutation: bundles are now inspected into a shared import preview before commit, and the control center surfaces a dedicated merge-review block for the currently inspected branch so import/merge actions are auditable before they mutate session state.
- Added explicit correction-target selection inside the Session Engine control-center timeline, so host corrections can now branch from a user-picked history event instead of always auto-targeting the latest stable event; the control snapshot now carries event ids/seq lines for correction-safe rows, and host-side regression coverage locks the selected-target payload through to the emitted `correction_added` fact.
- Extended Session Engine correction composition into the shared result/workspace entry path, so Quick / Balance / Mirror correction sheets can now load the current head-line timeline, optionally target a specific history event, and still fall back to the latest stable event without forcing users through the dedicated control center first.
- Promoted Session Engine correction branching into the shared action-bar contract, so Home / History / Self Portrait / Settings and the Quick / Balance / Mirror result shells now all route correction composition through one reusable `DecisionSessionEngineActionBarView` + `DecisionSessionEngineCorrectionComposerView` path instead of maintaining a separate result-only sheet flow.
- Promoted stalled-line recovery into the shared Session Engine shell action bar for surfaces that already know active-session health, so Home / History / Self Portrait / Settings can trigger host recovery without drilling into the control center while result cards still stay conservative until they carry explicit stalled-state facts.
- Added a service-level `DecisionSessionEngineReviewDigestBuilder` and routed both `DecisionSessionEnginePresentation` and the flight-deck data layer through it, so `Pending import draft / Merge review queue / Replay anchor ready` now come from one builder instead of parallel string logic in the panel and runtime export paths.
- Added a shared `DecisionSessionEngineImportPreviewDigestView` and reused it in both the Session Engine import-preview sheet and the compact panel, removing the last duplicated import-digest block across the recovery UI surfaces.
- Corrected runtime replay/recovery semantics so `DecisionSessionRuntimeInspectionSession.replayRecoverySummary` no longer reuses `mergeReviewLine` as a fake kill-switch line; shared replay/export surfaces now omit that field unless a real kill-switch source exists.
- Moved the control-center merge review card fully onto structured `detailRows`, so the merge review surface now renders the same source/target/checkpoint facts already carried by the snapshot instead of rebuilding those rows in the view.
- Added dedicated regressions for Session Engine review digest building, health-summary severity, structured merge-review detail rows, and replay-recovery kill-switch omission, tightening the shared recovery/review surface against future UI wording drift.
- Added shared `DecisionSessionEngineReviewDetailPresentation` plus `DecisionSessionEngineReviewDetailListView`, so import-preview and merge-review surfaces now render the same labeled fact rows from service-level presentation instead of each view hand-formatting its own support lines.
- Added a shared `DecisionSessionEngineReviewDigestView`, moving the panel-level `Review digest` section out of inline view code and onto a reusable Session Engine review surface component.
- Expanded Session Engine review regressions to pin import-preview detail rows, merge-review detail rows, and the negative-path case where the shared review digest must not invent merge/replay items when source facts are absent.
- Session Engine evaluation checkpoints now persist compact eBrain turn facts into runtime inspection: `eBrain budget / route / risk / permit / host gate / fold / review task` are extracted from the latest checkpoint summary, exposed through `DecisionSessionRuntimeInspectionSession`, and rendered by the shared panel/control-center digest instead of staying buried in checkpoint storage only.
- Added end-to-end and presentation regressions for the new eBrain checkpoint digest path, so `BeforeAppModel -> DecisionSessionEngine checkpoint -> runtime inspection -> shared panel/control center` now stays pinned by tests rather than relying on manual replay inspection.
- Promoted those compact eBrain checkpoint facts into `DecisionSystemFlightDeck` data signals as well, so runtime export and flight-deck consumers now see not just “a checkpoint exists” but also the protected `budget / route / risk / permit / review-task` envelope that survived into the latest recovery-safe checkpoint.

## v1.2 Stabilization Invariants

- `active checkpoint` and `review head` must remain distinct across runtime export, flight deck, Home, History, Portrait, Settings, watch, and widget surfaces.
- `BudgetFrame`, `HostProfile`, `RiskCard`, `ActionPermit`, `ThoughtFold`, and `UpdateTicket` remain registry-governed contracts with explicit compatibility, migration, and rollback metadata.
- `openEvolutionControl`, checkpoint apply/approve/rollback/clear-lineage, and host kill-switch policy must stay protected by dedicated regressions rather than only manual walkthroughs.
- Watch/widget surfaces may expose sanitized evolution pressure, but mutation always routes back into the iPhone control center rather than running inline on auxiliary shells.
- Mainline replay/history surface cleanup: added shared `DecisionReplayEntrySummaryView` and moved `ReviewProfileView`, `SelfPortraitView`, and `HistoryView` onto the same replay-linked entry shell instead of hand-assembling mode/status/title/summary wrappers around `DecisionReplayDiagnosticsBlockView`.
- Tightened host parity coverage so `recentReplayDiagnosticsPresentations(limit:)`, `replayDiagnosticsPresentation(matchingRecordID:)`, and `replayDiagnosticsPresentationsByRecordID(matching:)` all resolve the same summary/source/budget/task facts for a shared quick-history replay record.
- Reduced `HistoryDetailView` duplication by centralizing the shared `section header -> detail card -> replay diagnostics -> continuation actions` scaffold used by quick, balance, and mirror detail paths.
- Added shared `DecisionSessionEngineSessionSummaryView`, moving the repeated title/header-plus-digest shell out of both `DecisionSessionEnginePanelView` and `DecisionSessionEngineControlCenterView` so active/recent session summaries and inventory rows now sit on the same session-summary contract.
- Promoted `DecisionSystemFlightDeck.sessionEnginePresentation` into the service layer and added `DecisionSystemFlightDeckSessionEngineView`, so the portrait flight deck now surfaces the same Session Engine recovery-line contract consumed by the shared shell panels instead of falling back to layer reports alone.
- Collapsed the new portrait flight-deck Session Engine block back onto `DecisionSessionEnginePanelView` and added `DecisionSessionEnginePresentation.unattached`, removing another local fallback/presentation fork across Home, History, Settings, and Self Portrait.
- Added shared `DecisionSessionEngineSurfaceView`, so Home, History, Settings, and Self Portrait now reuse one `Session Engine panel + action bar` composition instead of each restitching the same recovery/correction surface with only local placeholder text differences.
- Added `DecisionSystemFlightDeck?.sessionEnginePresentationOrUnattached`, so Home, History, Settings, and Self Portrait now resolve the shared Session Engine fallback from one service-level helper instead of repeating local `?? .unattached` branches at every view edge.
- Promoted Session Engine bundle portability onto the same watchdog-safe tool lifecycle as other host actions: `exportSessionEngineSession`, direct bundle import, and staged bundle import now open a tracked step, emit heartbeats, and append `tool_call_started/finished` facts on the source recovery line whenever the source session still exists locally.
- Moved the remaining quick/balance/mirror replay-entry copy mapping into `DecisionReviewEngine`, adding shared timeline/recent-entry presentation builders so `HistoryView` and `ReviewProfileView` now render the same title/detail/action/timestamp semantics from service-level projections instead of keeping separate view-local mappings.
- Extended that shared review projection into `HistoryDetailView`, adding shared detail-header and detail-row builders in `DecisionReviewEngine` so quick/balance/mirror detail sheets no longer maintain a separate set of field labels, reopen copy, and empty-row filtering rules from the rest of the replay/history surfaces.
- Added shared `DecisionReplayEntryHeaderRowView`, so `HistoryView`, `ReviewProfileView`, and `SelfPortraitView` now reuse one replay-metadata header row for label/status/timestamp/accent rendering instead of each carrying its own HStack layout and styling rules.
- Wired the shared `Pressure latency/power/cache/thermal` runtime line into `DecisionSystemEBrainSummary`, so the top-level 13-layer flight-deck summary in Home and Self Portrait now speaks the same L1/L3 pressure language as Session Engine checkpoints and replay diagnostics.
- Added shared `DecisionEvolutionReplayCheckpointFacts` plus `DecisionEvolutionEBrainFactsBundle.dataLayerSignals`, so live replay budget/pressure/task lines and flight-deck data-layer fact signals now come from one service-level helper instead of being rebuilt independently in `DeveloperDecisionReplayBuilder` and `DecisionSystemFlightDeck`.
- Added a shared `DecisionEvolutionLineageSnapshot.factsBundle` entry point and moved both `BehavioralAISubstrateBridge` checkpoint summaries and `DecisionSystemFlightDeck` persisted-checkpoint summaries onto it, trimming another service-layer fork in the recovered L1/L3 runtime-fact path.
- Added shared `DecisionSessionCheckpointPresentationFacts` inside `DecisionSessionEngine` and moved `DecisionSessionEnginePresentation`, `DecisionSessionEngineControlSnapshot`, and `DecisionSystemFlightDeck` checkpoint digests onto it, so runtime line, decision/task line, pressure line, audit/kill-switch line, and action line now stay aligned across panel, control center, and flight deck.
- Moved `BeforeAppModel.sessionEngineCheckpointDraft` onto `DecisionEvolutionSessionCheckpointFacts.applied(to:)`, so checkpoint summary mutation now comes from one shared L1/L3 helper instead of a host-local append sequence.
- Added `DecisionEvolutionEffectiveEBrainContext` to `DecisionTestingRuntimeExport` and routed flight-deck replay-recovery signals through `DecisionSessionEngineReplayRecoverySummary.digestLines`, removing another pair of small service-layer summary forks in the runtime-export/flight-deck path.
- Moved recovered-checkpoint console summary assembly in `BehavioralAISubstrateBridge` onto `DecisionEvolutionEBrainFactsBundle.consoleRuntimeSummaryAdditions` / `consoleBrainSummaryAddition`, trimming the last local `runtime/pressure/brain` helper trio from the bridge path.
- Expanded `DecisionEvolutionEffectiveEBrainContext` to carry thought-fold, update-ticket, audit, and kill-switch facts too, so `DecisionTestingRuntimeExport.evolutionRuntimeFacts(...)` now reads one shared effective-eBrain context instead of rebuilding those arrays field-by-field.
- Added `DecisionEvolutionTurnDiagnosticsSupport`, moving live-turn `route / host / replay / audit / kill-switch / risk-factor / reason-code / ticket` assembly out of `BASEBrainTurnResult.diagnosticsPresentation` and into a shared service helper reused by replay diagnostics.
- Moved `BASEBrainTurnResult -> DecisionSystemEBrainSummary` and `DecisionEvolutionLineageSnapshot -> DecisionSystemEBrainSummary` conversion onto shared type extensions, so `DecisionSystemFlightDeckBuilder` no longer hand-builds separate live and persisted eBrain summary payloads.
- Shared `DecisionEvolutionEBrainPresentationSupport` now owns the lower-level `risk/permit/host` and `risk/permit/host/fold` recovery strings too, so `DecisionSessionEnginePresentation` replay-recovery digests and `BehavioralAISubstrateBridge` checkpoint output summaries no longer hand-assemble those lines independently.
- `DecisionEvolutionEBrainPresentationSupport` now also owns recovered-lineage copy plus `runtimeSummaryLine / brainSummaryLine / checkpoint calibration` phrasing, so `DeveloperDecisionReplayBuilder`, `DecisionEvolutionEBrainFactsBundle`, and `BehavioralAISubstrateBridge` no longer drift on the same persisted eBrain narrative.
- Persisted-checkpoint recovery titles and subtitles now also route through `DecisionEvolutionEBrainPresentationSupport`, so `DeveloperDecisionReplayBuilder`, `BehavioralAISubstrateBridge`, and `DecisionEvolutionCheckpointPresentation` share the same recovered-checkpoint wording instead of carrying separate mode-specific strings.
- Added `DecisionEvolutionCheckpointRecoverySupport` and moved workspace availability/empty recovery copy, operator active-checkpoint copy, and Session Engine timeline recovery/checkpoint-detail phrasing onto it, reducing another small fork between evolution workspace surfaces and the append-only session timeline.
- `DecisionEvolutionSourceDescriptor` checkpoint-recovery detail copy now also routes through `DecisionEvolutionCheckpointRecoverySupport`, keeping replay-source descriptors aligned with the shared persisted-checkpoint recovery language.
- Added shared Session Engine `recorded/compact` recovery-line helpers and moved panel/control-center versus flight-deck recovery-count phrasing onto them, so recoveries no longer drift between “latest recorded” and compact headline summaries.
- Added shared Session Engine inspection descriptor helpers for `checkpoint / open-step / branch` lines, moving panel/control-center and flight-deck session summary copy off local string assembly and onto one service-level contract.
- Added `DecisionEvolutionReviewPathPresentationSupport` and moved operator guidance, attention-signal review copy, and release-summary pending-review/recommended-kill-switch reasons onto it, reducing another service-layer fork in the evolution queue/review contract.
- Extended Session Engine inspection helpers to cover `checkpoint anchor` and `selected checkpoint` lines too, trimming another small fork between control-snapshot selection state and merge-review copy.
- Extended `DecisionEvolutionReviewPathPresentationSupport` to cover pilot guided-action headlines/details plus pending-review queue signal lines, and moved `DecisionEvolutionPilotControlSnapshot` and `DecisionEvolutionReleaseSummaryBuilder` onto those shared helpers instead of carrying separate queue/kill-switch/restorable-path strings.
- Added shared Session Engine recovery-status helpers for `watchdog expired`, `stalled recovery`, `paused recovery`, and `protected execution` copy, then moved `DecisionSessionRuntimeInspectionSession` and `DecisionSessionEngineHealthSummary` onto them so open-step alerts and recovery summaries no longer drift across runtime inspection and panel/control-center health states.
- Added `DecisionSessionReviewPresentationSupport` for `pending import / merge review / replay session / checkpoint detail` copy, moving `DecisionSessionEngineReviewDigestBuilder`, `DecisionSessionRuntimeInspectionSession.replayRecoverySummary`, and merge-review status lines off another small cluster of local string assembly.
- Added shared Session Engine inspection helpers for `activityLine` and `detailLine`, so runtime inspection and session presentation no longer each rebuild the same `open-step/stalled/recovery` summary shape by hand.
- Added shared `DecisionSessionEngineReviewDigestBuilder.presentations(...)` and `.signalLines(...)`, so Session Engine panel/control-center review items and flight-deck data-layer review signals now all derive from the same digest builder output instead of remapping the same lines in three different service paths.
- Added shared Session Engine inspection helpers for `eventLine / statusLine / headline / fallbackDetailLine`, moving another small copy seam out of runtime inspection, panel summaries, and control-center session summaries so they all resolve session status/detail wording through one service contract.
- Added shared Session Engine helpers for `stepSignalLine`, `mergeReviewSignalLine`, and `replaySessionRecordedLine`, so recent-session signals and replay-recovery summaries now resolve their zero-merge/open-step/recorded-session phrasing through the same service contract instead of keeping one last set of local fallbacks.
- Moved Session Engine import-preview manifest phrasing (`importPreviewHeadline`, import counts/head-branch lines, latest-checkpoint line, and exported-head branch status) into shared review helpers, so `DecisionSessionEngine.makeImportPreview(...)` no longer hand-assembles those strings separately from the import review presentation/tests.
- Moved Session Engine summary signal assembly for `pending import`, `replay recovery`, and `checkpoint digest` onto shared presentation helpers, so `DecisionSystemSessionEngineSummary.runtimeSummary(...)` / `.pendingImportSummary(...)` no longer keep their own local signal-formatting path for those three service contracts.
- Added shared Session Engine branch presentation helpers for `origin / detail / control-status / import-status` lines, moving another copy seam out of control-center branch summaries and import-preview branch previews so both paths now resolve branch wording from one service contract.
- Moved Session Engine import-bundle summary lines, import-review detail rows, merge-review route text, and timeline seq labels onto shared presentation helpers, so import preview, merge review, and control-center timeline rows no longer keep separate local wording for the same review/control facts.
- Added shared Session Engine timeline-detail helpers for merge/tool/checkpoint/step/session/branch event copy plus folded-merge notices, moving another local string seam out of `DecisionSessionEngine` so timeline rebuilds and replay-facing diagnostics stay on the same service contract.
- Added shared Session Engine control-review labels and empty-state copy, moving `control snapshot` empty messaging plus source/target/checkpoint row labels onto presentation helpers instead of keeping one more local control-center wording fork.
- Added shared Session Engine review-detail label helpers, so import-preview rows and control merge-review rows now resolve bundle/source/imported/checkpoint/branch/status labels and branch identity lines through the same presentation contract.
- Moved Session Engine digest-line construction for `pending import`, `merge review`, and `replay anchor` into shared review presentation helpers, so the digest builder now reads one service contract for both wording and severity instead of hand-assembling those three review facts locally.
- Added shared Session Engine `summarySignalLines(...)` generation, moving runtime and control-center summary signal assembly onto one presentation helper so flight-deck/runtime/control paths now expose the same inventory/branch/progress/review/replay/checkpoint signal stack.
- Added shared control-snapshot helpers for counts, selected-checkpoint anchor text, and selected-branches summary, removing another small set of local `ControlSnapshot` branches/if-else formatting seams.
- Added shared Session Engine inspection aggregation for fallback session/active/stalled/branch/checkpoint counts, so `controlCenterSummary` no longer recomputes those inspection-derived totals inline in two separate places.
- Moved Session Engine review-surface mapping helpers for import-branch rows, merge-review health summaries, review-detail rows, and digest-line item conversion into shared presentation support, so `ReviewPresentation` and `ReviewDigestBuilder` no longer keep their own lightweight conversion layer.
- Moved Session Engine control-merge review construction and review-digest signal-line projection into shared presentation helpers, so control snapshots and digest builders now share the same merge-review/detail-row and signal-line contract.
- Consolidated Session Engine runtime/control-center summary assembly behind shared `DecisionSystemSessionEngineSummary` builders for recent-session fallback, merge-count derivation, and signal generation, reducing another service-side duplication seam in flight-deck summary composition.
- Moved Session Engine control-branch and control-timeline item conversion into shared presentation helpers, so control snapshots no longer locally encode branch permission/detail rules or checkpoint-vs-event timeline row semantics.
- Added shared evolution-workspace runtime status and review-head notice helpers, moving Home’s `runtime spotlight vs attention fallback` choice plus `review head without active release path` copy out of the view layer and back into `DecisionEvolutionWorkspaceSnapshot`.
- Added shared release-summary presentation support, moving `active/review checkpoint headlines`, `active source`, `kill-switch text`, and mutation-hub operator copy out of `DecisionEvolutionReleaseSummaryView`’s local builder and into `DecisionEvolutionReleaseSummaryBuilder.swift`.
- Added shared pilot-control presentation support, moving `PilotControlPanel` header detail, embedded release-summary mode, pending-lineage note, and audit/kill-switch footer copy out of the view layer and into `DecisionEvolutionPilotControlSnapshot`.
- Added shared release-summary action support, moving `quick actions` gating plus rollback/approve/clear mutation intent selection out of `DecisionEvolutionReleaseSummaryView` and into `DecisionEvolutionReleaseSummaryBuilder.swift`, so mutation-hub and read-first release surfaces now share one action-row contract.
- Added shared operator-footer presentation support, moving `release summary / pilot control / checkpoint action bar / kill-switch panel` footer detail and button-style routing onto one surface-level contract instead of four repeated `OperatorActionFooterView` parameter bundles.
- Added shared navigation-row presentation support, moving `settings / pilot mutation hub / checkpoint mutation hub / operator footer` shortcut-button style and routing bundles onto one service contract instead of repeating `NavigationActionRow` configuration across evolution surfaces.
- Added shared evolution navigation and operator-summary support for `Home / History / Control Center`, moving read-first/control-center companion CTA bundles plus the control-center operator `mode/counts/kill-switch` digest out of the view layer and onto `DecisionEvolutionSurfaceContract` / `DecisionEvolutionOperatorSnapshot`.
- Added shared batch-mutation presentation support, moving the batch workspace header plus `mutation-hub empty selection` and `read-first operator guidance` copy out of `DecisionEvolutionBatchMutationPanel` and back onto `DecisionEvolutionSurfaceContract`.
- Added shared checkpoint-action presentation support, moving apply/approve-or-mark/clear-lineage gating plus read-first footer routing and mutation-hub navigation-row selection out of `DecisionEvolutionCheckpointActionBar` and onto `DecisionEvolutionSurfaceContract`.
- Added shared kill-switch-panel presentation support, moving the kill-switch header/detail, active-vs-recommended badge titles, recommended-policy summary, mutation button copy, and read-first footer routing out of `DecisionEvolutionKillSwitchPanelView` and back onto `DecisionEvolutionSurfaceContract`.
- Completed the operator-footer payload contract by moving footer `headline/detail` resolution plus the footer-owned navigation-row projection out of `DecisionEvolutionOperatorActionFooterView` and back onto `DecisionEvolutionSurfaceContract`, leaving the view as a pure renderer of shared presentation data.
- Added shared `DecisionEvolutionControlCenter` section presentations, moving the control-center workspace section `title/detail` narrative plus the release-readiness empty-state copy out of `DecisionEvolutionControlCenterView` and into `DecisionEvolutionSurfaceContract`.
- Added shared `DecisionEvolutionControlCenter` header and checkpoint-role presentations, moving the control-center header title/detail/refresh/navigation copy plus the shared `Active checkpoint` and `Review head` role labels out of `DecisionEvolutionControlCenterView`, `DecisionEvolutionControlSurfaceSummaryView`, and `DecisionEvolutionMutationPreviewView` and back onto `DecisionEvolutionSurfaceContract`.
- Added shared mutation-preview presentation copy, moving the preview badge title, control-surface impact title, `Current/Projected` row labels, section headings, and cancel copy out of `DecisionEvolutionMutationPreviewView` and onto `DecisionEvolutionMutationIntent`.
- Added shared mutation-outcome and pilot-impact presentation copy, moving outcome status/icon/targets/dismiss wording plus the pilot-impact strip header, target prefix, and `Active/Review` transition labels out of `DecisionEvolutionMutationOutcomeView` and `DecisionEvolutionPilotImpactStripView` and onto `DecisionEvolutionMutationIntent`.
- Added shared pilot-control surface copy, moving the pilot header title, guided-action section title, and guarded `restore/rollback/approve-queue/clear-lineage` button titles out of `DecisionEvolutionPilotControlPanel` and onto `DecisionEvolutionPilotControlSnapshot`.
- Expanded shared batch-mutation presentation support to own selector titles, `Targets:` prefix, and guarded batch action button copy, removing another layer of local wording from `DecisionEvolutionBatchMutationPanel`.
- Added shared checkpoint detail presentation support, moving rollback status/badge titles plus `Tickets/Audit/Kill switches/Diff/Recorded` copy and the default empty-lineage message out of `DecisionEvolutionCheckpointPanelView`, `DecisionEvolutionControlSurfaceSummaryView`, and `DecisionEvolutionCheckpointLineageDetailsView` and onto `DecisionEvolutionCheckpointPresentation`.
- Added shared checkpoint-selection and batch-selection summary support, moving the checkpoint-panel `Select/Selected` contract plus batch selection summary/targets lines out of `DecisionEvolutionCheckpointPanelView` and `DecisionEvolutionBatchMutationPanel` and onto shared evolution presentation helpers.
- Added shared checkpoint summary-badge and diff-bullet presentation support, moving `HIGH/DELAY/RECOVERED/ROLLBACK` badge assembly plus bullet-prefixed diff lines out of `DecisionEvolutionControlSurfaceSummaryView` and back onto `DecisionEvolutionCheckpointPresentation`.
- Added shared release-summary and pilot-control badge presentation support, moving `READY/WATCH/BLOCKED`, `PENDING`, `ROLLBACK READY`, and `LINEAGE-BACKED` badge assembly out of `DecisionEvolutionReleaseSummaryView` and `DecisionEvolutionPilotControlPanel` and back onto evolution service contracts.
- Moved release-summary mutation-hub operator copy and quick-action button titles (`Quick actions`, `Rollback active`, `Approve queue`, `Clear review lineage`) out of `DecisionEvolutionReleaseSummaryView` and onto `DecisionEvolutionReleaseSummaryBuilder` support helpers.
- Moved evolution-surface fallback copy for mutation transition placeholders (`none`), checkpoint-spotlight empty-state messaging, and navigation row default history/portrait titles onto shared mutation/surface support contracts so `MutationPreviewView`, `PilotImpactStripView`, `DecisionEvolutionControlCenterView`, and `DecisionEvolutionNavigationActionRow` no longer hardcode them locally.
- Centralized shared evolution-surface refresh titles and per-surface control-summary empty-state copy for `History`, `Settings`, `Control Center`, and `SelfPortrait` in `DecisionEvolutionSurfaceStatusPresentationSupport`, removing more local wording from those views.
- Moved `DecisionEvolutionOperatorSnapshot` surface/counts/kill-switch summary wording and `HistoryView` evolution-trail header/inventory copy into shared presentation helpers so operator summaries and history evolution headers no longer assemble those strings inline.
- Centralized runtime spotlight/review-head wording plus `Settings` read-first evolution header and `Control Center` dismiss copy into shared helpers, removing more local text assembly from workspace and surface views.
- Centralized mutation outcome status/icon/tone/targets formatting in shared intent support, removing the last local `Targets:` assembly from the outcome view.
- Centralized mutation preview `Current/Projected` checkpoint lines and pilot-impact `Targets` formatting in shared preview presentation helpers, trimming more local copy from evolution views.
- Routed `History` and `Self Portrait` checkpoint panel titles plus release-summary role titles back through shared checkpoint-role/runtime presentation helpers.
- Unified checkpoint lexicon (`none`, `Automatic`, `Review suggested`) so checkpoint presentation, operator summary, mutation intent, and batch mutation all read the same shared wording.
- Centralized release-path rollout headlines and reasons (`blocked/watch/ready`) in shared support so release summary no longer carries its own hardcoded headline ladder.
- Centralized shared evolution action titles (`apply/approve checkpoint`, `rollback active`, `clear review lineage`, `approve/mark/clear selected`) so release summary, mutation intent, and surface contracts no longer duplicate them.
- Centralized control-surface titles (`Evolution Control`, `Evolution control center`, `Kill-switch control plane`) plus shared `Approve review queue` wording so control interaction, operator summary, pilot control, and surface contracts now read one lexicon.
- Centralized repeated mutation-factory phrases (`automatic active slot remains`, `review queue will be emptied`, `active checkpoint remains`, and kill-switch removal warnings) plus additional shared action titles, reducing more service-side copy drift.
- Pulled more mutation-factory transition phrasing into shared support, including active-checkpoint handoff, automatic-slot movement, review-head shift/remain wording, and queue-retained approval copy.
- Centralized checkpoint role and rollback lexicon (`Active checkpoint`, `Review head`, `Rollback ready/unavailable`, `ROLLBACK READY/WATCH`) so runtime, release, pilot, and checkpoint surfaces consume one shared contract.
- Moved the History evolution queue-vs-history filter semantics into shared workspace support, so `HistoryView` now consumes `DecisionEvolutionHistoryFilter` and workspace-level filtered queue/history helpers instead of owning that split locally.
- Unified shared evolution summary badges for `PENDING`, `ROLLBACK READY`, and `LINEAGE-BACKED`, so pilot-control and release-summary surfaces consume the same badge builder.
- Unified mutation-hub headline/detail and queue-action titles (`Mutation hub`, `Quick actions`, `Approve queue`, `Restore/Rollback active path`, `Clear queue lineage`) across control interaction, release summary, and pilot control contracts.
- Added deeper shared evolution guidance support for `kill switches`, `pending review`, and `restorability`, letting review-path and release-path ladders consume the same underlying reason/detail contract.
- Moved release-path queue/audit/runtime-guardrail/guarded-ready stage copy behind shared guidance helpers, so `DecisionEvolutionReleaseSummaryBuilder` no longer owns that last headline/reason ladder locally.
- Unified the remaining kill-switch review/release headlines and details (`Watching recommended kill switches`, blocked review-path copy) so attention, review-path, and release-path surfaces now reuse one shared kill-switch lexicon.
- Centralized read-first versus mutation-hub routing copy for evolution surfaces, so control-interaction and pilot-control shells consume one shared navigation-detail contract.
- Moved control-center batch-selection ordering/deduping into shared selection support, and unified mutation-surface `Targets`/`Active` labels so batch, preview, and outcome copy no longer drift independently.
- Tightened evolution support coverage with direct assertions for pending-review guidance helpers and multi-variant navigation-row presentation builds, reducing drift risk across shared routing contracts.
- Centralized the remaining attention fallback copy (`rollback-ready active checkpoint`, `Evolution is quiet`) so attention surfaces no longer keep those phrases as view-local literals.
- Moved the remaining `History` evolution trail queue/history section copy and filter label into `DecisionEvolutionHistoryTrailPresentationSupport`, so `HistoryView` now consumes shared queue/history presentation data instead of hardcoding those section semantics.
- Added shared `Home` evolution review-queue presentation support for headline/detail, queued badge, and pending-count summary lines, so `HomeView` now renders that queue card from shared presentation instead of assembling the copy inline.
- Unified `operator` and `pilot` fallback blocker-priority calculation behind shared `DecisionEvolutionPrimaryBlockerEvaluator` workspace/control-surface helpers, so those two service paths no longer keep separate local ordering logic when release-summary guidance is absent.
- Moved control-surface summary active/review section payload construction into `DecisionEvolutionControlSurface.summarySectionPresentations`, so `DecisionEvolutionControlSurfaceSummaryView` now renders shared role titles and source-aware badge stacks instead of re-deriving checkpoint-summary semantics locally.
- Promoted `DecisionEvolutionActiveCheckpointSource` into shared domain lexicon support, so checkpoint-recovery messaging and widget snapshot source titles now reuse the same pinned/recovered active wording instead of maintaining separate mappings.
- Centralized shared `Active/Recommended kill switches` line builders in `DecisionEvolutionKillSwitchPresentationSupport`, so release-summary presentation, eBrain diagnostics facts, and session replay recovery no longer each format those strings independently.
- Added typed widget evolution presentation support for compact status copy, control-entry titles/system images, and iPhone handoff instructions, so `WidgetEvolutionSnapshot` now derives review/control/rollback/open wording from one shared contract instead of branching on raw string literals.
- Added shared `WidgetEvolutionSurfacePresentation` badge/control-entry payloads, so the iPhone widget and watch glance now consume one typed evolution surface contract instead of each stitching together badges, headlines, compact-status text, and handoff actions from scattered snapshot fields.
- Replaced raw widget/watch attention-severity branching with shared typed `DecisionEvolutionWidgetAttentionSeverity` normalization, so widget snapshots now collapse invalid severity IDs to `none` and both compact-status/control-entry decisions consume one typed attention contract instead of string literals.
- Moved primary-blocker guidance selection for evolution operator snapshots and pilot guided actions behind shared `DecisionEvolutionPrimaryBlockerPresentationSupport`, so mutation/read-first operator copy and pilot next-step routing no longer keep separate blocker-to-headline/detail switch ladders.
- Moved release-summary blocker state/headline/reason selection behind the same shared `DecisionEvolutionPrimaryBlockerPresentationSupport`, so operator, pilot, and release-path evolution guidance now all consume one blocker-to-guidance contract instead of maintaining a third switch ladder in `DecisionEvolutionReleaseSummaryBuilder`.
- Moved evolution attention-signal severity/badge/headline/detail assembly behind shared `DecisionEvolutionAttentionPresentation`, so queue/kill-switch/rollback fallback ordering now consumes the shared primary-blocker evaluator instead of keeping another local if/else ladder in `DecisionEvolutionAttentionSignal`.
- Centralized pilot-control `Review audit:` and `Suggested kill switches:` line builders in shared pending-review/kill-switch support, so `DecisionEvolutionPilotControlSnapshot` no longer carries its own last two queue-state formatting helpers.
- Added shared lineage presentation support for pending-review notices and retained approval copy, so `DecisionEvolutionPilotControlSnapshot` plus pending/selected mutation previews no longer each format `lineage-backed` queue wording independently.
- Moved repeated mutation transition wording (`review-suggested -> automatic`, checkpoint enters review queue, selected/pending queue movement) into shared `DecisionEvolutionMutationPhraseSupport`, so checkpoint/pending/selection mutation previews no longer each keep their own queue-transition sentence variants.
- Broke a real replay-builder recursion loop by moving live-turn replay checkpoint budget/pressure/task extraction behind `BASEBrainTurnResult` helpers, so `DeveloperDecisionReplayEBrainSummary(turn:)` no longer re-enters `turn.replayCheckpointFacts` while building its own facts bundle.
- Refreshed `DeveloperDecisionReplayBuilderTests` to assert shared inspection/checkpoint/data-layer contracts from live fixture values instead of stale literals, and re-ran the real simulator `DeveloperDecisionReplayBuilderTests` suite to green after the recursion fix.
- Preserved host kill-switch policy when `DecisionTestingRuntimeExport` has no live or persisted eBrain context, so `flightDeck.releaseControlSummary` still carries blocking kill-switch facts instead of collapsing to an empty policy surface.
- Added a narrow `DecisionSessionEngine.resetForTesting()` path and wired `BeforeAppModelSessionEngineTests` through async setup/teardown, so shared session-engine SQLite state no longer leaks stalled steps across full-suite simulator runs.
- Split release-path blocker guidance into explicit shared `releaseState`, `releaseHeadline`, and `releaseReasons` helpers inside `DecisionEvolutionPrimaryBlockerPresentationSupport`, so the remaining operator/release service contract no longer depends on one large monolithic switch for release summary semantics.
- Moved mutation-intent `message / preview headline / preview summary` copy behind shared `DecisionEvolutionMutationIntentCopySupport`, and added shared review-path guidance builders for queue-kill-switch and pending-review operator/pilot states, so mutation preview plus operator/review-path surfaces no longer keep their last service-side headline/detail branches inline.
- Centralized the remaining batch-mutation, checkpoint-action, and kill-switch control labels into dedicated shared lexicons, so mutation-hub panels no longer keep inline button/header strings for `Select all`, `Apply selected`, `Mark checkpoint`, `Clear checkpoint lineage`, `Apply recommended`, `Clear active switches`, and the read-first kill-switch header detail.
- Centralized settings read-first, history trail, home queue, and control-center section narratives into shared surface lexicons, so evolution header/section title-detail copy no longer lives inline inside the surface-contract builders.
- Centralized history-trail inventory wording and operator-summary labels (`Active/Review/Pending/Rollback-ready`, `Kill switches:`) into shared support, so evolution surface summaries no longer assemble those count prefixes inline.
- Centralized release-summary checkpoint headline formatting and `Active source:` wording in shared presentation support, so release surfaces no longer inline that last checkpoint/source label assembly.
- Centralized history-filter titles and the checkpoint `Lineage pending…` fallback summary behind shared presentation support, so workspace filters and checkpoint fallback status copy no longer keep their last inline literals.
- Split widget evolution compact-status tokens, badge titles, and iPhone control-entry copy into dedicated shared lexicon support, so widget/watch evolution surfaces no longer keep `Pending/Rollback/Active switches/Recommended`, `P/R/K`, or review/control/rollback/open handoff wording hidden inside formatter branches.
- Added shared evolution narrative formatting support for `a • b` joins, `prefix: a • b` lines, and `N checkpoint(s) ...` phrasing, then routed pending-review, kill-switch, operator-summary, release-summary, history/home inventory, runtime spotlight, and checkpoint-detail copy through it so those surfaces stop re-implementing the same string assembly in parallel.
- Routed the remaining eBrain runtime/brain/checkpoint budget-route/kill-switch joins plus replay diagnostics `Budget/Pressure/Factors/Reason codes` through shared narrative-formatting support, and moved release-summary `Active source:` onto that same formatter contract so replay/evolution service layers stop hand-assembling their last repeated bullet and prefix lines.
- Routed the remaining Session Engine and replay-side bullet/prefix assembly through shared narrative and review presentation helpers, so pending-import details, merge-review details, replay recovery summaries, checkpoint fact lines, bridge session details, and replay `Audit/Kill switches` lines no longer hand-assemble their last local `•` joins or `prefix: a • b` strings.
- Removed the last raw `joined(separator: " • ")` tails from widget snapshot presentation and replay-builder assertions, so widget/watch compact-status assembly and replay data-layer expectations now both consume shared formatter/kill-switch support instead of leaving any bare bullet-join literals behind.
- Suppressed hosted unit-test transient presentation state at app bootstrap and skipped lifecycle bootstrap while the test host is active, so simulator-backed `xcodebuild test` runs no longer emit the recurring `Currently, only presenting a single sheet is supported.` warning before suites begin.
- Skipped shared app-group container URL probing during test-host storage resolution, and locked both behaviors with focused tests, so simulator test logs now stay free of both the app-group entitlement warning and the root-sheet presentation noise.
- Added a typed `DecisionEvolutionPolicyEngine` seam for action availability and pilot-route recommendation, so release-summary quick actions and pilot guided actions now consume one shared mutation-capability contract instead of separately recomputing rollback/approve-queue/lineage routing from raw control-surface facts.
- Stored typed release-summary `primaryBlocker` facts in the shared policy input path, and added focused policy tests for workspace normalization, legacy headline fallback, restore-only mutation surfaces, and pending-review mutation routing, so evolution release/pilot/operator/attention behavior now has tighter cross-surface regression coverage.
- Added typed checkpoint-action eligibility and checkpoint-set eligibility to `DecisionEvolutionPolicyEngine`, so checkpoint action bars and control-center batch selection now reuse one shared policy contract for apply/approve/mark/clear-lineage gating instead of rechecking approval/apply/lineage state in multiple services.
- Rewired checkpoint mutation intents and batch-selection intent builders to consume those shared policy helpers, and added focused tests for read-only selection behavior plus ordered review/automatic/lineage/restorable buckets, so checkpoint and batch mutation eligibility now stays aligned across service-level intent generation and presentation support.
- Moved typed mutation preview projection state into `DecisionEvolutionPolicyEngine`, rewired `DecisionEvolutionMutationIntent` to consume shared current/projected active-review state instead of re-deriving transitions inline, and locked both focused preview-state tests plus control-surface parity integration tests so mutation previews and post-mutation evolution state stay aligned across policy consumers.
- Added shared workspace-level `policy(...)` accessors and threaded a single `DecisionEvolutionPolicyOutput` through `DecisionEvolutionSurfaceState`, so operator guidance, attention signals, and widget snapshot release-state/source export now consume one evaluated policy contract instead of re-evaluating workspace state through separate local paths.
- Added a typed widget control-entry recommendation on `DecisionEvolutionPolicyOutput` and persisted that explicit entry kind in widget evolution snapshots, so watch/widget CTA selection (`Review/Control/Rollback`) now follows the same shared policy contract as the app surfaces instead of being re-derived only from raw snapshot counts.
- Added shared `surfaceActionPlan(...)` on `DecisionEvolutionPolicyOutput`, rewired pilot-control guided actions and release-summary action-row visibility/title routing to consume it, and locked parity tests so those two surfaces no longer drift on approve-queue vs navigation recommendations.
- Added shared `DecisionEvolutionWorkspaceMutationActionBundle`, rewired pilot-control and release-summary action consumers to reuse the same restore/rollback/approve-queue/clear-lineage intents, and locked mutation-intent tests so those surfaces no longer rebuild workspace action bundles independently.
- Added shared `BeforeAppModel.performEvolutionMutation(...)` intent execution routing, rewired pilot/release/checkpoint/batch mutation consumers to confirm through that single executor, and updated evolution-engine preview-sync tests so cross-surface mutation confirmation no longer duplicates `intent.kind -> appModel` switch logic in multiple views.
- Persisted a typed widget control-entry presentation from shared policy output during app-model snapshot sync, sanitized and stored that CTA payload in shared widget state, and updated widget/watch tests plus evolution-engine parity checks so widget surfaces prefer the shared stored `Review/Control/Rollback` entry contract instead of reconstructing CTA copy from legacy snapshot heuristics.
- Extended the shared widget control-entry payload with a persisted `triggerReason`, routed both home-widget intents and watch handoffs through that same stored CTA contract, and updated queue/widget/evolution tests so `openEvolutionControl` now carries the same prompt-plus-reason context across widget/watch entry paths instead of splitting reason handling between local surface fallbacks.
- Added shared widget `primaryActionPresentation` fallback/output on `WidgetSnapshot`, rewired lock-screen and circular widget families to route through the same typed evolution-control CTA when attention is active, and locked widget/evolution parity tests so accessory widget actions no longer hardcode a local quick-open button while medium/small surfaces use the stored shared control-entry contract.
- Added shared widget/watch evolution-control routing helpers on `OpenEvolutionControlIntent` and `WatchHandoffCoordinator`, rewired medium/small widgets, accessory-widget primary actions, and watch evolution surfaces to consume the same typed payload, and locked queue-level tests so widget/watch entry paths no longer each rebuild `prompt/triggerReason` handoff routing inline.
- Added a shared `DecisionIntentEnvelope.openEvolutionControl(...)` factory plus `EntrySource`-level source-surface/default-trigger helpers, rewired widget intents and watch handoff routing to consume that one envelope builder, and locked fallback-trigger tests so widget/watch evolution-control launches no longer keep separate `.openEvolutionControl` queue construction paths.
- Added shared `DecisionIntentEnvelope.quickCapture(...)`, `openMode(...)`, and `reopenTomorrowItem(...)` factories, rewired app intents plus watch handoff helpers to consume them, and expanded queue-level tests so quick/open/reopen launches no longer keep parallel local envelope construction branches beside the shared evolution-control factory.
- Added shared `DecisionIntentEnvelope.predictiveIntervention(...)` and `resumeCurrentDecision(...)` factories plus an explicit `sourceSurface` override for deferred reopen envelopes, then rewired bridge/current-brain/queue tests to consume those factories and locked trim + notification-surface behavior so predictive/resume/deferred envelope semantics no longer drift between shared domain helpers and bridge-level test fixtures.
- Added shared predictive-notification presentation support so `NotificationService` now prefers candidate `title/detail` and only falls back to shared product-language risk copy when that copy is blank, with focused tests locking identifier, fallback copy, and delivery-delay behavior.
- Added shared `PendingLaunchRequest` launch factories plus a shared `sanitizedPrompt` contract, rewired launch-request store and resolver to consume that single prompt-cleaning path, and updated launch/store tests so the legacy pending-launch stack no longer keeps its own duplicate trim semantics beside the intent-envelope flow.
- Added shared `DecisionIntentEnvelope.routedInput(...)` plus `PendingLaunchRequest.decisionIntentEnvelope`, extended the BAS Apple entry-intent runtime with typed `routedInput` support, and rewired app/bridge lifecycle pending-launch consumers to execute through `consumeDecisionIntentEnvelope(...)`, so the old pending-launch runtime executor is no longer a separate app-side behavior seam beside the shared entry-intent path.
- Added `PendingLaunchRequestStore.consumeDecisionIntentEnvelope()` and rewired bridge/app lifecycle consumers to accept deferred `DecisionIntentEnvelope` values directly, so legacy pending-launch state is now threaded only as an envelope source instead of surviving as a separate execution-time request type in app-side lifecycle routing.
- Moved launch-request resolution onto shared `DecisionIntentEnvelope` prompt/mode/scenario semantics, added a shared sanitized prompt-seed contract, and kept `PendingLaunchRequest` as a forwarding adapter only, so legacy launch resolution no longer owns its own parallel parsing rules beside the shared envelope path.
- Promoted `PendingLaunchRequestStore` to envelope-native read APIs with `consumeEnvelope()` and normalized envelope-queue decoding, rewired app lifecycle consumption to use that shared read seam directly, and left legacy `PendingLaunchRequest` readers as forwarding adapters only.
- Added envelope-native `enqueue`/`set` overloads on `PendingLaunchRequestStore` and pushed queue persistence down to a shared stored-envelope plus protected-prompt path, so legacy `PendingLaunchRequest` writes now forward into the same protected queue seam instead of owning a separate request-only write path.
- Added typed `DecisionSessionCheckpointExecutionCapability` persistence on session-checkpoint eBrain anchors, threaded that persisted capability frame through checkpoint facts, runtime export, flight-deck summary attachment, and replay diagnostics, and locked focused tests so live and recovered L1-L5 execution capability lines now survive across session checkpoint, export, and replay surfaces instead of disappearing when only persisted checkpoint state is available.
- Added `executionCapabilityLine` to `DecisionEvolutionEBrainFactsBundle` and taught runtime export to rehydrate it from either live execution capability frames or persisted checkpoint anchors, then locked Package C parity tests so recovered host-gate facts plus L1-L5 capability lines now stay aligned across checkpoint-only runtime export and evolution control-surface recovery paths.
- Added typed `executionTierID` and `foundationTierID` onto capability-coverage reports, threaded those values from runtime export / flight-deck parity into the shared BAS coverage builder input, and locked Package D tests so L1-L5 capability coverage now carries the same execution-capability surface contract as runtime export and checkpoint-anchor flight-deck inspection.
- Added shared `DecisionIntelligenceCoordinator.runtimeCoordination(...)`, rewired testing runtime snapshots to persist the resulting profile-plus-status pair, exposed a native `executionCapabilityFrame` on `DecisionTestingRuntimeSnapshot`, and redirected runtime export to consume that snapshot-level capability frame on the non-persisted path, so coordinator/runtime-snapshot/runtime-export L1-L5 capability facts now flow through one runtime spine instead of being recomputed in parallel.
- Added a shared `BeforeAppModel.runtimeSnapshot` seam, rewired app-model provider status/descriptors and local-model library presentation to consume that one testing runtime snapshot, and reused the same snapshot for substrate inspection, session-checkpoint execution capability, replay turn capture, and runtime export fallback paths, so app-side L1-L5 runtime surfaces now stay aligned with the coordinator/runtime-export spine instead of rebuilding their own local status snapshots.
- Added shared retrieval-mode helpers onto `DecisionIntelligenceRuntimeCoordination` and rewired `BehavioralAISubstrateBridge` current-brain priming/refresh routing to consume them, so bridge-side current-brain retrieval behavior now follows the same typed L1-L5 runtime coordination contract as runtime snapshots instead of pulling execution profiles directly.
- Added a shared `statusesByKind -> runtimeCoordination` overload on `DecisionIntelligenceCoordinator`, extended `DecisionIntelligenceProviderPipeline.runtimeStatus(...)` with an injectable device capability snapshot, and rewired pipeline runtime status to consume that shared coordinator seam, so provider-pipeline status now follows the same L1-L5 runtime spine as app, bridge, and testing snapshots without losing deterministic test coverage for explicit non-simulator capability profiles.
- Added shared `strategy(for:)` and `allowFallbacks` accessors onto `DecisionIntelligenceRuntimeCoordination` and rewired `DecisionIntelligenceCoordinator` reminder plus quick/balance/mirror refinement paths to consume them, so the coordinator no longer pulls a parallel `executionProfile(...)` inside those last assistive/refinement branches and now follows the same runtime-coordination spine end to end.
